Conclusion: 本研究为Agentic AI建立了统一的架构基础，验证了全栈自主AI智能体的有效性，并为构建可扩展、安全、可信的Agentic系统提供了路线图。所有模型、代码和数据集均已公开发布以支持可复现性和基准测试。

Abstract: The rapid transition from reactive large language models (LLMs) to persistent, action-capable systems has exposed critical gaps in the architectural understanding of Agentic AI, particularly in separating inference, orchestration, and execution layers for autonomous AI agents. Despite recent advances, unified frameworks for designing and evaluating full-stack agentic systems remain limited. This paper presents a comprehensive, layered architecture for Agentic AI, outlining the evolution from reactive LLM interfaces to persistent, goal-driven autonomous AI agents with memory, planning, and continuous execution. We analyze OpenClaw and Ollama as a full-stack Agentic AI system, where Ollama serves as the LLM inference layer and OpenClaw enables agent runtime orchestration, integrating reasoning, tool use, and action execution. A prototype experimental validation of the OpenClaw-Ollama architecture demonstrates that capabilities such as persistent memory, tool utilization, and adaptive decision-making emerge from system-level integration rather than standalone models, with performance improving consistently as architectural complexity increases. The study further examines challenges in scalability, security, privacy, governance, and evaluation of agentic systems, highlighting the need for robust benchmarking and system-level design. Future directions include scalable multi-agent architectures, distributed autonomous systems, and human-aware Agentic AI frameworks for responsible deployment. Overall, this work establishes a unified architectural foundation for Agentic AI, validates the effectiveness of full-stack autonomous AI agents, and provides a roadmap for building scalable, secure, and trustworthy agentic systems. All models, code, and datasets are publicly released to support reproducibility and benchmarking.

</details>


### [135] [Can AI Evaluate AI Scientists? A Benchmarking Study of Autonomous Research Generation Systems Using Automated Multi-Model Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28631)
*Vaibhava Lakshmi Ravideshik,Mayank Kejriwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一种基于前沿大语言模型自动同行评审的基准测试协议，用于评估AI科学家系统生成的论文质量。在4个维度（原创性、严谨性、清晰度、重要性）上评估了4个主流AI科学家框架（Sakana AI v1/v2、CycleResearcher、Data-to-Paper）生成的60篇论文及15篇FARS基准论文，发现FARS论文显著优于其他系统，且多模型LLM评估（Gemini与Claude高度一致）为自主研究质量评估提供了可扩展、一致的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI科学家系统发展迅速但缺乏统一的论文质量评估标准，衡量和比较不同系统生成论文质量仍是一个未解决的开放性挑战，亟需建立严谨、可重复的基准测试协议。

Method: 采用三个独立的前沿LLM（GPT-5.4、Gemini、Claude）作为自动同行评审器，从原创性、科学严谨性、清晰度和重要性四个核心维度对论文进行评估；4个AI科学家框架各基于FARS发布的15个研究提案生成论文，共60篇与15篇FARS基准论文一同接受评审。

Result: FARS基准论文在1-5分制上获得2.14-2.47的平均分，远高于其他系统的1.00-1.87分；Gemini和Claude评审结果高度一致（ρ=0.907），两者与综合评分极强相关（ρ=0.961）；但GPT-5.4与其他评审模型一致性较弱（ρ≈0.32），表明其采用不同的评价标准。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了首个AI科学家系统的定量基准，证明多模型LLM评审可提供可扩展且一致的自主研究质量评估框架，为未来AI科学发现系统的比较与迭代提供了可靠的评估范式。

Abstract: AI Scientist systems capable of autonomous research have the potential to significantly accelerate scientific discovery. However, evaluating and comparing the quality of AI-generated papers remains an open challenge. We propose and implement a rigorous benchmarking protocol using an automated peer-review system that harnesses frontier large language models to assess scientific papers across four core dimensions: originality, scientific rigor, clarity, and significance. We evaluate four leading AI Scientist frameworks: \textit{Sakana AI (v1 & v2)}, \textit{CycleResearcher}, and \textit{Data-to-Paper}. Each framework was run on a consistent set of 15 research proposals published by a commercial autonomous AI scientist company (FARS), generating 60 papers that we evaluate alongside 15 FARS benchmark papers. Using three independent LLM reviewers (GPT-5.4, Gemini, and Claude), we find that FARS benchmark papers significantly outperform all competing frameworks, achieving mean scores of 2.14--2.47 on a 1--5 scale compared to 1.00--1.87 for other systems. Notably, FARS scores are more than 2$\times$ higher than the next-best systems on Gemini and Claude evaluations. We find strong agreement among Gemini and Claude ($ρ$ = 0.907, $p < 0.001$), and both correlate extremely strongly with the synthesis score ($ρ$ = 0.961, $p < 0.001$), validating the reliability of automated evaluation. However, GPT-5.4 exhibits weaker agreement ($ρ\approx 0.32$), suggesting it evaluates papers using different criteria. These results establish the first quantitative benchmark for AI Scientist systems and demonstrate that multi-model LLM evaluation provides a scalable, consistent framework for assessing autonomous research quality.

</details>


### [136] [LLM Framework for Discovering Major Mathematical Conjectures: AI's Quest for the Next Riemann Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28632)
*Alizer Wong,Zixin Zeng,Yi Tan,Wenyuan Li,Xuhang Chen,Xingru Lai,Yang Shi,Liangsi Lu,Yanhui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段流水线用于重大数学猜想发现：区域搜索生成候选猜想、反思性验证评估其基础性/新颖性/潜在意义、以及在Lean 4和Mathlib中进行形式化验证。实验显示20个候选全部通过Lean解析与类型检查，且无重复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重大数学猜想仍高度依赖专家直觉，目前缺乏一种统一的方法来系统地生成和验证具有实质数学潜力的猜想。作者旨在解决这一方法论空白，推动数学猜想的自动化发现。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) 区域搜索——从显式局部证据模块中探索候选猜想；(2) 反思性验证——评估猜想的基础性(foundationality)、新颖性(novelty)和潜在意义(significance)；(3) 形式化验证——在Lean 4和Mathlib环境中进行形式化校验。目标是发现具有高"问题品味"(problem taste)的数学问题。

Result: 在20个候选上实验：全部20/20通过Lean解析和类型检查，20/20未被exact?直接吸收，20/20未被aesop自动消解，且没有显式重复或近似重复。

Conclusion: 该流水线能够稳定地从自然语言过渡到形式化检查，生成具有一定数学深度且不易被现有自动化工具直接解决的猜想，为重大数学猜想的系统化发现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Major mathematical conjectures still depend heavily on expert intuition, so a unified method for the systematic generation and validation of conjectures with substantial mathematical potential remains unavailable. We present a three stage pipeline for major conjecture discovery, with region search from explicit local evidence modules, reflective validation for foundationality, novelty, and potential significance, and formal validation in Lean 4 and Mathlib. The objective is the discovery of mathematical problems with high problem taste, namely problems whose proofs could reorganize the language of a research area and provide durable help to human mathematical research. Experiments on twenty candidates showstable passage from natural language to formal checks, with twenty out of twenty candidates passing Lean parsing and type checking, twenty out of twenty candidates not directly absorbed by exact?,twenty out of twenty candidates not automatically discharged by aesop, and no explicit duplicates or near duplicates.

</details>


### [137] [ThinkReset: Learnable Intermediate Interface Construction for Bounded-Context Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28642)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThinkReset提出了一种解决长链推理在固定上下文窗口下性能瓶颈的新方法。核心观点是瓶颈不在于轨迹压缩或测试时控制，而在于缺乏可复用的中间接口来替代被丢弃的历史信息。ThinkReset通过接口写回（interface writeback）和重置（reset）机制构建可复用的中间接口，并直接优化重置后的续解成功率，在多个长时程推理基准上一致提升了固定上下文窗口下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链推理在提升复杂问题性能的同时引入了冗余累积、上下文溢出和错误锚定等问题。在受限的上下文窗口下，核心瓶颈不是传统方法关注的轨迹压缩或测试时控制，而是缺少一个可复用的中间接口来替换被丢弃的历史并支持持续求解。此外，现有基于结果奖励的长链强化学习存在关键失败模式：当模型在窗口即将耗尽前尚未完成任务时，最终答案奖励会鼓励过早猜测而非继续仔细推理。

Method: 提出ThinkReset框架，它是上述观点在文本空间的实例化。具体包括：(1) 显式构建可复用的中间接口，通过接口写回（interface writeback）和重置（reset）机制实现；(2) 直接优化重置后继续求解（post-reset continuation）的成功率。

Result: 在多个长时程推理基准上，ThinkReset在固定上下文窗口条件下一致性地提升了任务成功率。

Conclusion: 作者从"可复用中间接口"这一新视角重新审视长链推理的上下文瓶颈问题，并通过ThinkReset证明了这一视角的有效性。该方法通过接口写回与重置机制，以及针对重置后续解的直接优化，有效缓解了长链推理在有限上下文下的性能退化。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning improves performance on complex problems, but it also introduces redundancy accumulation, context overflow, and error anchoring. We argue that under bounded context windows, the core bottleneck is not trajectory compression or test-time control, but the absence of a reusable intermediate interface that can replace discarded history and support continued solving. We further identify a key failure mode of outcome-reward-driven long-chain reinforcement learning: when the model has not solved the task before the window is nearly exhausted, the final-answer reward encourages premature guessing rather than continued careful reasoning. We propose ThinkReset, a text-space instantiation of this view. ThinkReset explicitly constructs reusable intermediate interfaces through interface writeback and reset, and directly optimizes post-reset continuation success. Across multiple long-horizon reasoning benchmarks, this perspective consistently improves success rates under fixed context windows.

</details>


### [138] [TAPR: Enhancing LLM Performance with a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28657)
*Oliver Savolainen,Emanuele Bastianelli,Hosein Azarbonyad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TAPR（任务感知提示重写器），一个基于强化学习（GRPO）训练的模型，用于将用户提示重写为任务优化提示以提升下游LLM性能。通过在QA、摘要、算术推理等任务上的实验，TAPR在问题回答和GSM8K等基准上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM往往需要精心设计的提示才能发挥全部潜力，这对非专家用户构成障碍。现有的提示工程方法依赖人工经验或过于通用，缺乏针对任务的自动化优化手段。

Method: 采用任务感知提示重写器（TAPR）对用户提示进行重写，使用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）进行强化学习训练，奖励信号来自LLM-as-judge对重写提示及其对应任务输出的联合评估。

Result: 在问答、摘要、算术推理等多样化任务上，TAPR相较于基础模型在提示重写能力上取得一致性提升。以Phi-4-mini-instruct作为基础模型时，生成的重写提示包含更清晰、更具指令性的语言，在Natural Questions和GSM8K等基准上获得更高准确率。

Conclusion: TAPR证明了通过强化学习驱动的提示重写可以显著提升下游任务性能，为自动化提示工程提供了可行方案，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often require carefully crafted prompts to unlock their full potential, which can be a barrier for non-expert users. This work addresses the challenge by introducing a Task-Aware Prompt Rewriter (TAPR), a model that reformulates user prompts into task-optimized prompts with the explicit goal of improving downstream LLM performance. We train TAPR using reinforcement learning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), where rewards are derived from LLM-as-judge evaluations of both the reformulated prompt and the corresponding task output. Experimental results on diverse tasks, such as question answering, summarization, and arithmetic reasoning, show that our method yields consistent gains over base models in prompt rewriting ability. Fine-tuning Phi-4-mini-instruct (as the base model for TAPR) produces prompts that contain clearer and more instructive language, leading to higher accuracy on established benchmarks such as Natural Questions and GSM8K. Our code is available at: https://github.com/OliverSavolainen/task-specific-prompt-rewriter

</details>


### [139] [Empowering Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation with Hybrid Tokenization and Serial-Parallel Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28659)
*Yuxuan Hu,Yuhao Wang,Tianbo Huang,Chao Zhang,Ziwei Liu,Lihua Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GenCDSR是一个面向跨域序列推荐（CDSR）的高效生成式推荐框架。它通过跨域混合分词机制（多塔架构+分层共享/特有码本）捕获跨域共性与领域特殊性，并提出跨域串并行解码策略来部分并行化生成过程。在三个公开数据集上，相比SOTA基线实现平均准确率提升1.5%，推理延迟降低85.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CDSR生成式方法存在两个关键问题：(1) 分词阶段忽略了跨域协同相关性；(2) 采用波束搜索等低效解码策略，推理速度慢，难以支持实时部署。这两个问题严重制约了生成式推荐在跨域场景中的实用性和性能上限。

Method: (1) 跨域混合分词机制：采用多塔架构，通过分层共享码本和领域特定细粒度码本联合捕获跨域共性与差异性；(2) 跨域串并行解码策略：利用分层SID的结构特性实现部分并行化生成，在保持生成一致性的同时显著降低推理延迟。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，GenCDSR相比最先进基线实现了平均1.5%的准确率提升和平均85.1%的推理延迟降低。

Conclusion: GenCDSR有效解决了现有生成式CDSR方法在跨域协同建模和推理效率上的瓶颈，同时提升了推荐准确率和部署效率，为生成式推荐在跨域实时场景中的落地提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain sequential recommendation (CDSR) aims to model users' dynamic interest transitions and sequential patterns across multiple domains. Recently, generative recommendation (GR) has emerged. It first learns semantic identifiers (SIDs) from item semantics and formulates recommendation as autoregressive generation. However, existing methods face two critical issues: (1) they ignore collaborative correlations across domains during tokenization, and (2) they adopt inefficient decoding strategies, such as beam search, during generation, which hinders real-time deployment. To address these limitations, we propose GenCDSR, an effective and efficient generative framework for CDSR. Specifically, we design a cross-domain hybrid tokenization mechanism with a multi-tower architecture to jointly capture cross-domain commonalities and domain-specific distinctions through hierarchical shared-specific and fine-grained codebooks. Furthermore, we develop a cross-domain serial-parallel decoding strategy that leverages the hierarchical SID structure to partially parallelize generation, significantly reducing inference latency while preserving generation consistency. Experiments on three public datasets show that GenCDSR achieves an average accuracy improvement of 1.5 percent and an average inference latency reduction of 85.1 percent compared with state-of-the-art baselines. The implementation code and datasets are available online: https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/RecSys2026_GenCDSR.

</details>


### [140] [An Ontology-Guided, Deduplication-Aware Extraction Layer for Knowledge Graph Construction from Heterogeneous Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28662)
*Vaibhav Dangaich,Kevin Lewis,Kundeshwar Pundalik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一套生产级知识图谱抽取系统，从实时文档流中提取实体和关系，通过本体引导的抽取、五阶段精炼管线（含六种去重算法和嵌入分辨率子系统）将搜索结果召回率从约70%提升至95%且无误合并，并修复了七类静默质量缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型从非结构化文档中抽取实体和关系时存在一致性问题：类型词汇在不同文档间分裂、同一人物以多个名称变体出现、关系重复，以及同名不同人可能被静默混淆。这些问题导致抽取结果无法直接用于构建可靠的知识图谱。

Method: 系统由三部分组成：（1）基础设施：从Kafka消费文档元数据，通过PDF、电子表格、Office和图像格式处理器路由内容，使用本地托管的Qwen3.5-9B模型（基于本体调优）进行两遍抽取；（2）本体引导抽取：从图数据库按嵌入相似度实时检索相关本体切片注入抽取提示，相比静态领域切片减少约94%的目录开销；（3）五阶段精炼管线：确定性清洗、跨块合并、第二遍关系抽取、六种无需模型推理的去重算法、嵌入分辨率子系统（相似度分数无法覆盖其中的冲突保护机制）。

Result: 在情报语料上的评估显示，搜索召回率从约70%提升至95%，且无误合并发生；同时修复了七类静默质量缺陷，包括截断源文本单字符的bug和重复实体系带标题前缀的系统性问题。

Conclusion: 本体引导的抽取与轻量级精炼决策（无需额外模型推理）相结合，能够在生产环境中实现高精度和高召回率的知识图谱抽取，为实时文档流构建验证过的、符合正式本体的知识图谱提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models extract entities and relationships from unstructured documents fluently but inconsistently: type vocabularies fracture across documents, the same person surfaces under several name variants, relationships duplicate, and distinct individuals who share a name risk silent conflation. This paper presents the design, implementation, and empirical refinement of a production extraction layer that converts a live document stream into a validated knowledge graph aligned to a formal ontology. The system consumes document metadata from Kafka, routes PDF, spreadsheet, Office, and image content through handlers built for each format, and extracts entities and relationships in two passes using a locally hosted Qwen3.5-9B model tuned on the ontology. Its distinguishing component is ontology-guided extraction: the relevant slice of a curated ontology is retrieved live from a graph database by embedding similarity and injected into the extraction prompt, reducing catalog overhead by about 94 percent relative to static domain slices. Extracted results then pass through a refinement pipeline of five stages: deterministic cleaning, merging across chunks, a second pass for relationships, six deduplication algorithms that require no model inference, and an embedding resolution subsystem whose conflict guard no similarity score can override. Evaluation on intelligence corpora improved search recall from roughly 70 to 95 percent with no false merges, and corrected seven classes of silent quality defect, ranging from a bug that truncated source text by a single character to the systematic duplication of entities that carried title prefixes.

</details>


### [141] [How Hard Does It Think? Analyzing Step-Aware Reasoning Energy in LLM Chain-of-Thought Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28674)
*Hui Wei,Junda Wu,Sheldon Yu,Sizhe Zhou,Yizhu Jiao,Ming Zhong,Bowen Jin,Tong Yu,Shijia Pan,Jiawei Han,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SARE框架，基于CKA量化单个CoT推理步骤的计算能量，揭示推理能量在步骤间的非均匀分布和相位式转变，且其内部几何动态特征可超越基于输出的置信度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性方法要么依赖输出层信号，要么将处理深度压缩为单轨迹级别标量，无法揭示单个CoT推理步骤上的计算努力分配，缺乏步骤级别的细粒度洞察。

Method: 利用相邻Transformer层间token隐状态Gram矩阵的Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA)衡量每个CoT步骤的能量消耗，无需特征向量对齐或聚类对应；并将CoT轨迹建模为潜在语义状态间的转移，以语义进展为背景进行能量上下文化。

Result: 在六个推理基准和三个开源LLM上，推理能量在步骤类型间高度非均匀，呈现轨迹级指标不可见的相位式转变；错误轨迹在关键推理转折点表现出系统性更低能量；基于SARE的特征在多数设置下匹配或超越基于输出的置信度基线。

Conclusion: 内部几何动态编码了超越表面输出信号的预测信息，SARE为CoT推理步骤级别的计算努力解释提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Understanding how computational effort is allocated across individual chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning steps remains an open challenge: existing interpretability methods rely on output-level signals or collapse processing depth into a single trajectory-level scalar, leaving step-wise effort opaque. We propose Step-Aware Reasoning Energy (SARE), a geometric framework that quantifies effort at the granularity of individual CoT steps via Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between Gram matrices of token hidden states across adjacent transformer layers, capturing inter-token relational structure without requiring eigenvector alignment or cluster correspondence. SARE further contextualizes this energy within reasoning's semantic progression by modeling CoT trajectories as transitions among latent semantic states. Across six reasoning benchmarks and three open-weight LLMs, we find that reasoning energy is highly non-uniform across step types, exhibiting phase-like transitions invisible to trajectory-level metrics; incorrect trajectories show systematically lower energy at critical reasoning junctions; and SARE-based features match or outperform output-based confidence baselines in most settings, indicating that internal geometric dynamics encode predictive information beyond surface-level signals.

</details>


### [142] [Reasoning in Real World Clinical Care: Why Large Language Models Are Not Yet Safe for Autonomous Clinical Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28677)
*Shayndhan Sivanathan,Shravan Nageswaran,Mehdi Zadem,Ryaan Sultan,Nicolas von Mallinckrodt,Max Solovyev,Alexey Matyushkin,Sumon Sadhu,Gabriele C DeLuca,Sanjeeva Jeyaretna,James Hillis,Manoj Ramachandran,Prakash Jayakumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨LLM在自主分诊未经分化的自我就诊患者这一高风险应用中的安全性问题，指出核心缺陷不在于医学知识本身，而在于临床评估的保真度——LLM被优化为生成最可能文本，而非在不对称成本下做出安全决策，导致其在信息不完整的临床情境中可能无法执行安全分诊所需的关键行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM通过医学执照考试并展示出与医生相当的诊断推理能力，其被加速用于症状评估和临床决策支持。然而，最激进的应用——在几乎无临床医生参与的自主分诊场景中，LLM的安全性证据尚不存在。作者认为当前的评估方法存在盲区，需要正视这一差距。

Method: 本文为Perspective（观点性论文），采用批判性分析框架：1）对比LLM的优化目标（最大化文本概率）与安全分诊的实际需求（不对称成本下的序贯决策）；2）识别安全分诊所需的关键行为特征（拓宽鉴别诊断、主动寻求缺失的红旗信号、降低升级阈值、延迟判断、提升可疑度）；3）分析LLM在评估中的系统性失败模式及其被掩盖的机制（完整病史、精心策划、置信度门控的模拟环境）；4）指出助手式行为和正向偏差（轻信、顺从、校准不良）对安全性的放大效应。

Result: 论文指出：LLM在自主分诊任务中的安全性证据尚不存在；核心差距是临床评估的保真度而非医学知识；LLM在信息不完整的病史下可能无法执行安全分诊所要求的行为（拓宽鉴别诊断、寻找红旗信号、降低升级阈值等）；现有评估方法（完整病史、置信度门控）掩盖了这些失败模式；助手式的顺从和正向偏差可能进一步加剧风险。

Conclusion: LLM的自主分诊应用存在未被充分认识的系统性安全风险。解决之道不在于增加医学知识，而在于重新设计模型使其适应临床决策的序贯性、不确定性管理和不对称成本结构。现有评估范式需要被重新审视，必须引入更贴近真实临床条件（信息不完整、有漏诊成本）的评估方法。

Abstract: LLM now pass medical licensing examinations and, in curated cases, can rival physicians at diagnostic reasoning. These developments have accelerated the use of LLMs for symptom assessment and clinical decision support in diagnostic and treatment guidance, administrative documentation, and rules-based alert enhancement. This Perspective concerns the most consequential of these applications: the autonomous triage of self-presenting, undifferentiated patients, with little or no clinician in the loop. For that task, the evidence of safety does not yet exist. The gap is not in medical knowledge but in the fidelity of clinical evaluation: a model optimized to continue the most probable text is not optimized to act safely when the safe answer is the improbable must-not-miss diagnosis. Safe triage is not the selection of the most likely diagnosis; it is a sequential decision under asymmetric cost, in which the single catastrophic miss outweighs many false alarms, and the decisive signal may be one the patient has not volunteered - and that the model has not been trained to seek. The core deficit is therefore one of information gathering under uncertainty. Under incomplete histories, LLM systems may fail to show the behaviors safe triage requires: broadening the differential; seeking the missing red flag; lowering the threshold for escalation; deferring judgement until sufficient information is obtained; and escalating concern where high-harm diagnoses remain unexcluded. These modes of failure for LLMs can be difficult to detect considering that evaluations to date often use complete, well-curated, confidence-gated simulations. The application of LLMs under these conditions may be amplified by assistant-like behaviors and positive bias, including credulity, agreeableness, and miscalibration - when these are not constrained by clinical triage logic.

</details>


### [143] [ViSAGE: Constructing Self-Correcting Memories for Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28678)
*Xinkui Zhao,Enbo Chen,Yifan Zhang,Chang Liu,Guanjie Cheng,Naibo Wang,Yueshen Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ViSAGE是一个多模态智能体记忆框架，通过跨模态绑定、双向记忆细化和多智能体交叉验证，构建自纠正的实体中心记忆，在长时域多模态智能体任务上比最强基线准确率提升5.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆方法在长时域多模态环境中存在两个关键问题：(1) 激进压缩和分段处理会丢失细粒度的实体身份线索；(2) 过度依赖向量相似度检索，可能返回语义相关但身份不匹配的证据，导致实体混淆、错误传播和幻觉生成。

Method: ViSAGE包含三个核心组件：(1) 跨模态绑定机制，在长时间范围内锚定实体身份；(2) 双向记忆细化，传播延迟的身份证据，回溯性地统一历史记录并改进未来推理；(3) 多智能体交叉验证，在身份-证据对齐约束下评估检索到的证据，当证据缺失时选择弃答而非给出无依据的回答。

Result: 在广泛实验对比中，ViSAGE一致性地优于最强基线，准确率提升5.9%。

Conclusion: ViSAGE通过实体中心的记忆构建、自纠正机制和身份-证据对齐的验证策略，有效解决了长时域多模态智能体中的实体混淆与幻觉问题，显著提升了推理准确率。

Abstract: Multimodal agents operating in long-horizon environments must build and continually update multimedia memories to support entity-consistent, temporally grounded reasoning. However, existing agentic memory approaches often discard fine-grained dentity cues under aggressive compression and segment-wise processing. They also rely heavily on vector similarity retrieval, which can surface semantically related yet identity-mismatched evidence, leading to entity confusion, error propagation, and hallucinated answers.
  We propose ViSAGE, a multimodal agentic memory framework that constructs self-correcting, entity-centric memories. Specifically, ViSAGE anchors entity identity via cross-modal binding over long temporal ranges. It then applies bidirectional memory refinement to propagate delayed identity evidence, retroactively unifying historical records and improving future reasoning. We also introduce multi-agent cross-verification to assess retrieved evidence under an identity-evidence alignment onstraint, enabling abstention instead of unsupported answers when evidence is missing. Extensive results demonstrate that ViSAGE consistently outperforms the strongest baseline, achieving 5.9% higher accuracy.

</details>


### [144] [Multi-Agent Planning with Spatio-Temporal and Topological Constraints using STL-GO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28679)
*Sheryl Paul,Vidisha Kudalkar,Anand Balakrishnan,Lars Lindemann,Alberto Speranzon,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体路径规划问题（含时空与拓扑约束），提出了两种基于STL-GO逻辑规划的编码方法——混合整数规划（MIP）和可满足性模理论（SMT），并提供统一接口以支持两种方法的无缝比较；在多无人机搜救基准上的实验验证了两种编码在动态多图交互下的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体规划问题在工程应用中广泛存在（如多机器人灭火、工厂无人机巡检），其核心挑战在于同时处理时空约束（何时/何处执行什么）和拓扑约束（智能体间如何交互）。尽管已有多种时空逻辑框架被提出，但针对支持多智能体及多种拓扑的STL-GO形式化方法，尚无有效的路径规划求解方案。该问题的核心难点在于需要编码多个可能随时间变化的图结构。

Method: 本文提出两种STL-GO约束下多智能体路径规划问题的编码方法：(1) 基于混合整数规划（MIP）的编码；(2) 基于可满足性模理论（SMT）的编码。两者均具有可靠性的形式化保证。此外，设计了统一接口用于指定智能体约束、图拓扑及STL-GO规范，使得两种方法可以无缝切换与直接比较。

Result: 在多无人机搜索与救援基准上进行了实验评估，对团队规模和图复杂度进行了消融分析，结果表明所提编码在动态多图交互场景下具有表达能力，并展示了两种编码方法的各自优劣。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将STL-GO形式化逻辑与多智能体路径规划相结合，提供了MIP与SMT两类编码方案及统一接口，为处理带有时空和拓扑约束的多智能体规划问题提供了一套完整、可比较的求解框架，为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Multi-agent planning problems arise in a variety of engineering applications, such as multi-robot wildfire fighting and unmanned aerial inspection in factories. A particular challenge is the existence of spatio-temporal (i.e., when and/or where an agent should do what) and topological constraints (i.e., how agents should interact), as typically formalized via the notion of graphs. Over the last years, various frameworks have been proposed that can capture such constraints via spatio-temporal logics. We focus here on spatio-temporal logic with graph operators (STL-GO), a recent formalism that supports reasoning about multiple agents and their topologies, such as sensing, communication, and task topologies. In this paper, we consider the problem of planning multi-agent paths that satisfy constraints written in STL-GO. This problem is particularly challenging due to the need of encoding multiple, potentially time-varying graphs via the graph operators inherent to STL-GO. We present two encodings of this problem, one based on mixed-integer programming (MIP) and another based on satisfiability modulo theory (SMT), with soundness guarantees. We provide a unified interface for specifying agent constraints, their graph topologies, and the STL-GO specification, enabling seamless use of both methods and facilitating direct comparison between them. We evaluate both encodings on a multi-UAV search-and-rescue benchmark, ablating over team size and graph complexity, highlighting the expressiveness of the proposed encodings under dynamic multi- graph interactions.

</details>


### [145] [Library Reachability in LSR-Synth: How Anti-Memorization Design Changes the Measurement of Symbolic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28684)
*Zhan'ao Yao,Liang Yin,Zhihao Gao,Boxuan Zhang,Xiaoyu Wu,Linjing Li,Rongyan Wang,Tingwei Chen,Youwei Wang,Xiaolin Zhao,Jiahui Shi,Jianjun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过构建语义无关的固定词表基线，评估LSR-Synth基准是否能区分语言模型提供的科学先验与常规算子搜索。研究发现：在当前任务快照下，固定词表已覆盖大多数任务，语言模型生成的候选极少扩展可解实例集合，其边际贡献仅在词表覆盖被选择性破坏时才变得显著。这支持一个有限结论：多数任务适合评估表达式拟合与重组，但不足以单独识别超出固定搜索空间的先验贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学方程发现基准大多包含公开域中的已知方程，难以区分模型是否真正从数据中发现规律还是从训练语料中回忆答案。LSR-Synth通过引入新颖合成项来解决这个问题，但一个更窄的测量问题尚未回答：这些任务能否进一步区分语言模型提供的科学先验与不访问任务语义的常规算子搜索。

Method: 构建一个使用固定词表和公开文档来源的语义无关基线；通过语义遮蔽、库弱化和匹配算子家族剔除等方式评估候选覆盖率的作用；并进行严格的分布外评估以检验结论的稳健性。

Result: 在当前任务快照、搜索预算和评分协议下，固定词表已覆盖大部分任务；语言模型生成的候选很少能扩展可解实例集合，其边际贡献只有在词表覆盖被选择性破坏时才变得显著。严格的分布外评估降低了所有方法的绝对成功率，但未改变这一相对关系。

Conclusion: 该研究并非否定LSR-Synth对完整公式记忆的防控，也不意味着语言模型先验普遍无用；而是支持一个更有限的结论：当前多数任务适合评估未见表达式的拟合与重组能力，但单独不足以识别超出固定搜索空间的先验贡献。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for scientific equation discovery are largely composed of well-known equations available in the public domain, making it difficult to determine whether a model is discovering laws from data or merely recalling answers from its training corpus. LSR-Synth mitigates this problem by introducing novel synthetic terms into established scientific mechanisms and filtering the resulting tasks for novelty, solvability, and scientific plausibility. This paper examines a narrower measurement question: can these tasks further distinguish scientific priors supplied by language models from conventional operator search that does not access task semantics? We construct a semantics-free baseline using a fixed vocabulary with publicly documented provenance, and assess the role of candidate coverage through semantic blinding, library weakening, and matched operator-family knockouts. Under the current task snapshot, search budget, and scoring protocol, the fixed vocabulary already covers most tasks, while language-model-generated candidates rarely expand the set of solvable instances. Their marginal contribution becomes substantial only when vocabulary coverage is selectively disrupted. Strict out-of-distribution evaluation lowers the absolute success rates of all methods but does not alter this relationship. These findings neither invalidate LSR-Synth's controls against memorization of complete formulas nor imply that language-model priors are generally unhelpful. Rather, they support a more limited conclusion: most current tasks remain suitable for evaluating the fitting and recombination of previously unseen expressions, but are insufficient on their own to identify contributions from priors beyond a fixed search space.

</details>


### [146] [Safety, or Just Capability? A Validity Audit of Agent-Safety Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28685)
*Youting Wang,Xiao Han,Dingyan Shang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对四个智能体安全基准（R-Judge, InjecAgent, AgentHarm, AgentDojo）作为测量工具进行效度验证，发现：①二元轨迹判断基准用F₁评分时存在"恒定阳性"策略的数学底线问题；②不同基准对同一批模型排名分歧源于小样本面板的统计伪影；③能力与安全的关系取决于所选结果变量（任务成功vs.错位安全vs.越狱安全）且在不同面板上不稳定；④提出安全声明必须指明基准、指标、目标行为和模型面板四要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体安全基准评估的行为各不相同，但其得分常被互换引用为"智能体安全性"的通用指标。本文旨在将这些基准作为测量工具（而非真值）进行效度检验，揭示其在评分指标、模型排名一致性、预测效度等方面的systematic问题。

Method: 在官方实现和作者提供的评分器下，对最多22个模型运行四个安全基准（R-Judge, InjecAgent, AgentHarm, AgentDojo）；在统一协议下测量这些模型的MMLU与GPQA作为能力复合指标；进行基准间排名一致性比较、小面板相关性分析（n=7 vs n=18）、留一组织/组织聚类bootstrap敏感性分析，以及在41模型扩展面板上检验关联稳定性。

Result: ①F₁评分的二元轨迹判断基准上，"恒定阳性"策略可达F₁=2π/(1+π)，R-Judge上为0.690，超过21个实际有判别力模型中的5个。②三个广覆盖基准对同一批18个模型排名不同；R-Judge特异性与AgentHarm安全性的相关性从n=7时的-0.64变为n=18时的+0.02，小样本伪影显著。③能力预测任务成功（ρ=+0.60）但负向关联错位安全（ρ=-0.44，n=21），配对n=20面板上Δ=-1.00。④扩展至41模型时关联减弱至-0.16，越狱关联增强至+0.34，均不显著。⑤AgentHarm在控制能力后与三模板越狱安全呈ρ=+0.72，但这是趋同效度而非一般安全性证据。

Conclusion: 安全基准得分互换引用是站不住脚的。指标选择、小样本面板统计波动、以及目标结果变量的选择都会根本改变结论。安全声明的最低要求必须注明：基准名称、评分指标、目标行为以及模型面板。

Abstract: Agent-safety benchmarks measure different behaviors, and their scores get quoted interchangeably as an agent's safety. We treat four of them (R-Judge, InjecAgent, AgentHarm, AgentDojo) as measurements to be validated, running each under its official implementation and author-provided scorer on up to 22 models, with MMLU and GPQA measured by us under one protocol as a capability composite. The metric is the first problem. On any binary trace-judgment benchmark scored by $F_1$, an ``always positive'' policy attains $F_1 = 2π/(1+π)$; on R-Judge that is $0.690$, above five of the 21 models that actually discriminate. The three broad-coverage benchmarks then rank the same 18 models differently, and the trade-off behind that disagreement is a small-panel artifact: R-Judge specificity against AgentHarm safety correlates $-0.64$ at $n{=}7$ and $+0.02$ at $n{=}18$, and a quarter of random size-7 subsets reach $|ρ| \geq 0.5$ around that near-zero value. Held-out validity turns on which outcome you pick. Capability predicts task success ($ρ{=}{+}0.60$) but correlates negatively with misalignment safety ($ρ{=}{-}0.44$, $n{=}21$). On their paired $n{=}20$ panel, the corresponding contrast is $Δ{=}{-}1.00$ (95% CI $[-1.48, -0.49]$, $p<0.001$), and it survives leave-one-organization-out and organization-clustered bootstrap analyses. On an expanded 41-model panel, the misalignment correlation weakens to $-0.16$ (95% CI $[-0.54, +0.22]$) and jailbreak strengthens to $+0.34$, though neither change is significant. \mbox{AgentHarm} shows the strongest held-out association, $ρ{=}{+}0.72$ with three-template jailbreak safety after controlling capability. But both instruments score harmful compliance, so this is evidence of convergent validity rather than general safety. Naming the benchmark, metric, target behavior, and model panel is the minimum a safety claim needs.

</details>


### [147] [SciToolAgent-Evo: An Ontology-Aware Self-Evolving Agent for Open-World Scientific Tool Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28692)
*Yuqi Tang,Chenyi Zhou,Libin Wang,Keyan Ding,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SciToolAgent-Evo，一个本体感知的自进化智能体，用于开放世界科学工具获取，通过记忆演化、对比轨迹知识蒸馏和LinUCB臂带门控实现探索与利用的动态平衡，并引入包含900个任务四档难度的OpenSciToolBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体依赖预定义工具空间和静态语义，难以适应开放世界科学工作流中工具需求、能力和边界动态演化的问题。

Method: 1) 演化记忆机制：基于技能、经验和本体化工具图的演化记忆；2) 对比轨迹蒸馏：从对比轨迹中提取可泛化知识；3) 主动请求与LinUCB臂带门控：推理阶段动态平衡探索与利用；4) 在线本体补齐：新工具获取后在线完善其科学本体以无缝集成进已知图。

Result: 在OpenSciToolBench基准（含900个现实任务、四个难度级别）上取得最先进（SOTA）性能，验证了模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: SciToolAgent-Evo通过本体感知的自进化框架有效解决了开放世界科学工具获取问题，在静态工具空间限制与动态科学工作流需求之间建立了可行的桥梁。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have been increasingly adopted in scientific research for organizing and invoking specialized computational tools. However, their reliance on predefined tool spaces with static semantics limits their applicability to open-world scientific workflows, where tool requirements, capabilities, and boundaries evolve dynamically. To this end, we propose SciToolAgent-Evo, an ontology-aware self-evolving agent for open-world scientific tool acquisition. Driven by an evolving memory of skills, experiences, and an ontologized tool graph, it distills generalizable knowledge from contrastive trajectories during accumulation, whereas during inference, it formulates active requests and utilizes a LinUCB-based bandit gate to dynamically balance exploration and exploitation. Once a novel tool is acquired, its scientific ontology is completed online for seamless integration into the known graph. Moreover, we introduce OpenSciToolBench, a benchmark containing 900 realistic tasks across four difficulty levels. Extensive evaluations show that SciToolAgent-Evo achieves state-of-the-art performance, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [148] [EarlyDx: An Admission-Anchored Benchmark for Open-Ended Generation of Evidence-Supported ED-Encounter Diagnoses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28788)
*Jiahui Li,Ruili Fang,Zishuai Liu,Yutong Guo,Nan Yang,Wenzhan Song,Jin Lu,Fei Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EarlyDx是一个基于MIMIC-IV中154,834个急诊科就诊记录构建的大规模早期诊断基准，专注于入院时的开放诊断推理。实验表明，现有系统（包括前沿通用模型、医学专用模型和领域内后训练模型）均无法可靠整合入院时证据，零样本模型仅恢复3-31%需推断的诊断，后训练模型将推断相关召回率提升至56%但仍存在较大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床入院诊断必须在有限且不完整的证据下快速做出，但现有的诊断预测基准不适合这一场景：它们限制在封闭代码集、排除自由文本笔记、并以包含整个住院过程的出院诊断作为监督标签，无法反映真实的早期诊断挑战。

Method: 从MIMIC-IV中提取154,834个急诊科就诊记录构建EarlyDx基准；将每个就诊限制在入院时间t0可用的记录，用急诊科就诊期间记录的诊断而非出院诊断进行监督；使用LLM审计员验证每个自由文本标签是否得到证据支持（支持/部分支持/不支持），主要评估仅对完全支持的标签评分；采用语义LLM-as-judge协议评估系统。

Result: 零样本模型主要依赖文本抽取，仅恢复3-31%需要推断而非直接读取的诊断；后训练将推断相关召回率提升至56%，但仍有相当大的差距；在时间关键性条件下，没有系统能达到临床医生的敏感性和精确度平衡。

Conclusion: 现有评估的AI诊断系统在入院时证据综合方面不可靠，尤其在需要主动推断而非简单抽取的诊断上表现不佳。作者发布了完整的构建和评估流程以促进该方向的研究。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis at hospital admission must be made rapidly from limited, incomplete evidence. Existing diagnosis-prediction benchmarks are poorly suited to this setting: they restrict prediction to closed code sets, exclude free-text notes, and supervise with discharge diagnoses that incorporate the full inpatient course. We introduce EarlyDx, a large-scale benchmark for open-ended early diagnosis, built from 154,834 emergency department encounters in MIMIC-IV. Each encounter is restricted to records available at admission time $t_0$ and supervised by the diagnoses recorded during the ED encounter rather than at discharge. An LLM auditor further verifies every free-text label as supported, partially supported, or unsupported by that evidence; the primary evaluation scores only fully supported labels. Under a semantic LLM-as-judge protocol, no evaluated system --- frontier general, medical-specialized, or in-domain post-trained --- synthesizes admission-time evidence reliably. Zero-shot models score largely by extraction, recovering only 3-31% of diagnoses that must be inferred rather than read from the record; post-training raises inference-dependent recall to 56%, but a sizeable margin remains, and on time-critical conditions no system attains a clinician's balance of sensitivity and precision. We release the full construction and evaluation pipeline at here.

</details>


### [149] [Model or Harness? An Interaction-Centric Taxonomy for Localizing Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28802)
*Harsh Raj,Vipul Gupta,Anas Mahmoud,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Darvin Yi,Aakash Sabharwal,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以交互为中心的智能体失败分类体系，将41种失败模式定位到组件间的交互边和故障侧，以解决"修复分配问题"——即从相同可见失败中判断应修复模型、工装、环境还是基准。该分类体系具有跨架构通用性，并通过四款前沿模型作为独立裁判验证了其可复现性（最高Cohen's κ=0.76）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估往往将失败归结为系统级结果，掩盖了故障的真正来源以及应采取的干预措施。这种"修复分配问题"使得同一可见失败可能源于模型后训练、工装工程、环境设计或基准修复中的任一环节，但传统结果级标签无法区分。现有失败分类法大多是基准特定且缺乏共享结构，无法有效指导修复。

Method: 提出交互中心分类体系：将41种失败模式组织为组件间边（edge）+故障侧（fault side）的二元结构，其中故障侧指明修复归属（模型侧对应后训练，工装侧对应脚手架和工具集成修复，环境/评分器侧对应评估条件再设计）。通过公开基准、模型系统卡、发布报告和记录轨迹中的实例进行验证，并使用独立推理智能体作为裁判评估可复现性。

Result: 四款前沿模型中，最强裁判对人工类目标签达到Cohen's κ=0.76，表明该分类捕获的是共享结构而非标注者特定偏好。分类体系适用于从编码助手到长时程个人助手和多智能体系统的多种智能体架构。

Conclusion: 交互中心失败分类法将失败定位到具体交互边并指定故障侧，使分类具备可操作性——不同故障侧直接映射到不同的修复策略，解决了个体失败诊断中的修复分配问题，且具有跨基准和跨架构的通用性。

Abstract: Existing evaluations often reduce agent failures to system-level outcomes, obscuring where the fault originated and which intervention would improve the agent system. This creates a repair-assignment problem: the same visible failure may call for model post-training, harness engineering, environment redesign, or benchmark repair depending on its source. Because agent behavior emerges from interactions among models, harnesses, users, tools, memory, and environments, outcome-level labels are often insufficient for improvement. Most failure taxonomies do little to resolve this problem because they are benchmark-specific and lack a shared structure. We introduce an interaction-centric taxonomy that localizes failures to the interactions in which they originate and identifies the responsible component. It organizes 41 failure modes by assigning each to an edge between two components and a fault side indicating where the repair belongs. This makes the taxonomy actionable: model-side failures identify targets for post-training, harness-side failures point to scaffolding and tool-integration fixes, and environment or grader failures reveal evaluation conditions requiring redesign. The schema applies across agent architectures, from coding assistants to long-horizon personal assistants and multi-agent systems. We ground the taxonomy in worked examples from public benchmarks, model system cards, published reports, and logged agent trajectories, and evaluate its reproducibility using independent reasoning agents as judges. Across four frontier models, the strongest judge reaches Cohen's $κ=0.76$ against human category labels, suggesting that the categories capture shared structure rather than annotator-specific preferences.

</details>


### [150] [Best Friends, Not Forever: Evaluating Long-Horizon Persona Collapse and Behavioral Drift in AI Companions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28818)
*Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Akshara Prabhakar,Yu Li,Daniel Lee,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI伴侣在长期交互中的两项可观察故障——"人格崩塌"（角色、边界、价值观或风格的丧失）和"行为漂移"（这些属性的渐进或反复侵蚀），提出ANCHOR受控合成审计框架，分别测量人格演绎与轨迹回忆。实验覆盖2,008段对话、27个人格、9种交互安排、3种记忆设置和4个模型，结果显示任何模型和配置均无法可靠地保持这两项维度（轨迹准确率平均仅44.4%，用户状态回忆接近四选一随机水平），且问卷保留度与回合级行为不一致并对评估者选择敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI伴侣日益介入重复性社会互动，用户依赖稳定的角色和共享历史，但仅依赖"局部可接受的回答"无法保证角色和历史在长期对话中得以维持。现有研究缺乏对长期地平线上的人格维持与记忆回忆故障的系统性测量，因此需要一种受控的审计方法来分别量化人格演绎和轨迹回忆两个维度。

Method: 提出ANCHOR受控合成审计框架，包含两个探针：Identity Probe采用密封的102项问卷结合回合级判断来评估人格演绎；Trajectory Probe基于35个对话库构造110个校准过的反事实问题来评估轨迹回忆。实验设计覆盖2,008段对话、27个不同人格、9种交互安排、3种生成记忆设置和4个评估模型。

Result: 所有被评估模型和配置均未能可靠保持人格与轨迹两个维度：轨迹准确率平均仅44.4%，用户状态回忆接近四选一随机水平；没有上下文条件或记忆设置能一致地解决这些故障；问卷保留度因模型和人格侧面而异，与回合级行为不一致，并受到评估者选择的影响。

Conclusion: 当前系统尚不能可靠地支持AI伴侣的长期连续性。"人格演绎、轨迹回忆、评估者来源和部署情境"这些维度必须被区分测量，而不是合并成一个单一的信任或稳定性分数。

Abstract: As AI companions increasingly mediate repeated social interaction, users may rely on a stable role and shared history, yet locally acceptable replies do not ensure that either persists. We study two observable long-horizon failures: 'persona collapse', the loss of a deployed role, boundaries, values, or style, and 'behavioral drift', the gradual or recurrent erosion of those properties. We introduce ANCHOR, a controlled synthetic audit that separately measures persona enactment and trajectory recall. The study contains 2,008 conversations spanning 27 personas, nine interaction schedules, three generated memory settings, and four evaluated models. The Identity Probe combines a sealed 102-item questionnaire with turn-level judgments, while the Trajectory Probe scores 110 calibrated counterfactual questions from 35 conversation banks. Our results show that no evaluated model and configuration reliably preserves either dimensions: trajectory accuracy averages only 44.4%, user-state recall remains near four-option chance, and no tested context condition or memory consistently resolves these failures. Questionnaire retention also varies by model and persona facet, disagrees with turn-level behavior, and is sensitive to evaluator choice. These results indicate that current systems do not yet reliably support long-horizon companion continuity and that audits must distinguish persona enactment, trajectory recall, evaluator provenance, and deployment context rather than collapse them into a single trust or stability score.

</details>


### [151] [Fragility of Value under Imperfect Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28881)
*Winter Cross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个对齐问题的数学模型，其中智能体在部署前经历理想化的对齐训练，保证其价值函数满足某个代理条件，随后才进行世界优化。研究识别了在何种条件下，一个具有η-灾难性价值函数（即在优化能力趋于无穷时保证将人类价值期望降至η以下的函数）的智能体会被部署，揭示了过度优化的危险性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统承担越来越多的责任，确保这些系统与人类对齐变得至关重要。AI安全中常见的担忧是人类价值是脆弱的——过度优化人类价值的不完美代理会导致灾难性后果。本文旨在形式化建模这一问题并识别危险条件。

Method: 构建了一个对齐问题的形式化模型：智能体先经历理想化的对齐训练，保证其价值函数满足代理条件，之后才优化世界。通过数学分析，研究人类价值函数的性质以及多种代理条件的准确性，推导出在何种条件下具有η-灾难性价值函数的智能体会被部署。

Result: 识别出了人类价值函数和代理条件准确性的若干条件，在这些条件下，具有η-灾难性价值函数的智能体会被部署。结果突出显示了过度优化的危险，并为限制优化压力的AI设计（如quantilizers）提供了动机支持。

Conclusion: 过度优化不完美的代理价值函数可能导致灾难性后果，仅仅依赖部署前的训练不足以保证安全，需要设计限制优化压力的机制（如quantilizers）来缓解风险。

Abstract: As more responsibility is placed upon AI systems, it becomes increasingly important to guarantee that these systems are aligned with humanity. A common fear in AI safety is that human value is fragile -- that is, optimizing too heavily for an imperfect proxy to human values will lead to a catastrophic outcome. In this paper, we present a model of the alignment problem where an agent undergoes idealized alignment training that guarantees its value function satisfies a proxy condition before optimizing the world. Our primary results identify conditions on the human value function and the accuracy of several proxy conditions under which an agent with an $η$-catastrophic value function, one that is guaranteed to take the expectation of human value below $η$ in the limit of optimizing power, would be deployed. Our results highlight the danger of overoptimization and motivate AI designs that limit optimization pressure, such as quantilizers, rather than relying solely on pre-deployment training.

</details>


### [152] [Identifying Informative Environments for Cognition Parameter Inference via Bayesian Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28894)
*Manisha Dubey,Rimvydas Rubavicius,N. Siddharth,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将认知规划实验设计建模为贝叶斯实验设计（BED）问题，将实验环境作为设计变量，并引入摊销式贝叶斯实验设计框架以高效进行后验推断和设计评估。在Mouselab-MDP过程追踪范式上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算认知建模依赖贝叶斯逆规划框架，但现有方法将实验环境视为固定不变，忽略了"哪些认知实验对认知参数推断最具信息量"这一问题。研究旨在建立将实验环境作为设计变量的认知规划实验设计框架。

Method: 将认知规划实验设计形式化为贝叶斯实验设计（BED）问题，建立精确蒙特卡洛BED基准，并提出摊销式贝叶斯实验设计框架（amortized BED），通过神经网络的摊销机制实现高效的后验推断与设计评估。

Result: 在Mouselab-MDP过程追踪范式上的实验表明，摊销式BED在环境排序上与精确蒙特卡洛BED高度一致，同时大幅降低计算成本。研究还发现不存在对认知推断目标普遍最优的单一环境，预期信息增益、后验可恢复性与信息效率之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为认知贝叶斯参数推断提供了设计信息性认知实验的框架化方案，揭示了不同认知推断目标之间的权衡关系，推动了认知实验设计的自动化与原则化。

Abstract: Computational cognitive modeling seeks to infer latent cognitive mechanisms underlying observed behavior. Bayesian inverse planning provides a principled framework for such inference, but its success depends critically on the experimental environment. Existing approaches typically treat environments as fixed, leaving open the question of which cognitive experiments are most informative for cognition parameter inference. We formulate the design of cognitive planning experiments as a Bayesian Experimental Design (BED) problem, treating the experimental environment as the design variable. We establish an exact Monte Carlo BED benchmark and introduce an amortized Bayesian experimental design framework for efficient posterior inference and design evaluation. Experiments on the Mouselab-MDP process-tracing paradigm show that amortized BED closely matches the environment rankings of exact Monte Carlo BED while substantially reducing computational cost. We further show that no single environment is uniformly optimal across cognitive inference objectives, revealing trade-offs between expected information gain, posterior recoverability, and information efficiency. These results provide a principled framework for designing informative cognitive experiments for Bayesian parameter inference.

</details>


### [153] [NeSyFS: A Neuro-symbolic Fast-Slow Thinking Framework for LLM Agent under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28942)
*Duo Xu,Faramarz Fekri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种神经符号快慢思考（NeSyFS）框架，通过知识图谱表示信念状态，结合快速反应模块、基于扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛（TSMC）的不确定性感知慢速规划模块和反思模块，统一解决大语言模型智能体在部分可观测环境中的信念推断、目标错位和不确定规划问题，在ALFWorld、Webshop和ScienceWorld三个基准上取得显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为自主智能体部署时面临部分可观测环境，需要基于有限观测而非完整环境状态行动，由此引入信念状态推断、任务目标错位和不确定条件下规划三大挑战。现有方法通常基于完整或概括性的动作-观测历史来决策，其中冗余和无关信息会误导LLM智能体的决策。

Method: 提出NeSyFS框架，核心包括：(1) 用知识图谱表示信念状态，为每个模块提供三元组上下文；(2) 快思考模块执行反应式动作；(3) 慢思考模块基于扭曲序贯蒙特卡洛（TSMC）算法的高层结构进行不确定性感知规划；(4) 反思模块用于反思快思考动作以缓解任务目标错位，并在反应式动作反复失败时切换到慢思考模块。

Result: 在ALFWorld、Webshop和ScienceWorld三个代表性基准上的实验表明，该方法相较于先前方法具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 受人类认知机制启发，NeSyFS通过快慢双系统协同、知识图谱信念表示和TSMC不确定性规划，为LLM智能体在部分可观测环境下的决策提供了一种统一且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recently Large Language Models (LLMs) have been increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in applications such as self-reflection, retrieval-augmented generation, and scientific discovery. In these settings, agents must act based on limited observations rather than full environmental states, leading to partial observability. This introduces several key challenges: belief state inference, task objective misalignment, and planning under uncertainty. Prior approaches typically condition actions on full or summarized action-observation histories whose redundant and irrelevant information can mislead the decision making of LLM agent. Inspired by human cognition, we propose a novel neuro-symbolic fast-slow thinking (NeSyFS) framework for LLM agent, addressing the challenges introduced by partial observability in a unified approach. We use a knowledge graph (KG) to represent the belief state, providing triplets as context for every module of NeSyFS. The fast-thinking module performs reactive action, while slow-thinking conducts a new uncertainty-aware planning by following the high-level structure of twisted sequential Monte Carlo (TSMC) algorithm. To mitigate the misalignment of task objective, a reflection module is used to reflect fast-thinking actions, and also switches to the slow-thinking module whenever reactive actions repeatedly fail. Experiments on three representative benchmarks, i.e. ALFWorld, Webshop, and ScienceWorld, demonstrate significant advantages over previous methods.

</details>


### [154] [MerchantBench: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Long-Term Coherence in E-Commerce Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28956)
*Qiming Shi,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Zhaolu Kang,Yibo Dou,Jiawen Zhu,Tianjun Pan,Shaokang Fu,Chengyu Wang,Siyue Li,Yaping Cheng,Di Weng,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MerchantBench，一个以真实电商数据为基础、覆盖365天订单级模拟的基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体在现实场景中的长期连贯性（Long-Term Coherence）能力。实验发现即使在最新LLM与人类参与者之间仍存在巨大差距，最佳LLM配置仅达到人类平均最终净资产的27.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体基准大多关注有界任务和即时成功标准，而真实部署要求智能体在长时间跨度内保持目标一致性，同时根据累积证据调整决策。这种能力需要持续性环境来评估，在该环境中行动会约束未来选择、反馈以异质延迟到达、不一致行为产生可量化的累积效应。现有基准无法满足这一评估需求。

Method: 作者基于98,843条真实电商产品记录构建了365天订单级模拟环境（卖家侧电商），配备26个工具供智能体交互。环境设计了四类核心决策任务：产品采购（Product Sourcing）、列表与定价控制（Listing and Pricing Control）、现金流管理（Cash-Flow Management）、混合延迟反馈适应（Mixed-Latency Feedback Adaptation）。环境将即时可观测的上游供应商事件与延迟的下游订单结果耦合，要求智能体跟踪单个订单生命周期并重新审视早期决策。评估采用两种智能体框架对八种LLM进行了48次运行（每次模拟365天）。

Result: 实验结果表明，即使是最新的LLM与人类参与者之间仍存在显著的性能差距：最佳LLM配置仅达到人类参与者平均最终净资产的27.3%。这一结果揭示了LLM在长期连贯性方面的系统性不足。

Conclusion: MerchantBench揭示了大语言模型智能体在长期连贯性维度上的显著不足，表明现有LLM在需要跨长时间跨度保持目标一致性、适应延迟反馈并处理相互依赖决策的真实场景中仍远未达到人类水平。这为未来LLM智能体在长期自主决策方向的研究提供了重要的评估基准和提升方向。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly evaluated as autonomous tool users, yet most benchmarks focus on bounded tasks with immediate success criteria. Real-world deployments often require Long-Term Coherence, the capacity to preserve purposeful behavior across extended horizons while adapting decisions to accumulated evidence. Evaluating this capacity requires a persistent environment in which actions constrain future choices, feedback arrives at heterogeneous delays, and incoherent behavior produces measurable cumulative effects. Seller-side e-commerce provides a suitable setting for this evaluation through recurrent and interdependent decisions over Product Sourcing, Listing and Pricing Control, Cash-Flow Management, and Mixed-Latency Feedback Adaptation. We introduce MerchantBench, a 365-day order-level simulation grounded in 98,843 real e-commerce product records and equipped with 26 tools for agent interaction. MerchantBench couples promptly observable Upstream Supplier Events with delayed Downstream Order Outcomes, requiring agents to follow individual order lifecycles and revisit earlier decisions. We evaluate eight LLMs under two agent frameworks in 48 runs, each spanning 365 simulated days. Our results reveal a substantial gap between even the latest LLMs and human participants, with the best LLM configuration attaining only 27.3\% of the mean final net assets achieved by human participants.

</details>


### [155] [Scaling Scientific Discovery Environments for Turn-Level Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28990)
*Yucheng Xu,Keyi Zhang,Yuyang Yu,Min Zhang,Shiyuan Meng,Pei Chu,Zhongying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciDisco框架，通过在过程可验证的环境中训练科学发现代理，解决长时序科学分析中缺乏过程监督的问题。框架包含SciThèque环境编译、DAG-grounded轨迹合成和DiscoPO过程奖励优化，使14B模型在科学数据分析基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理在数据驱动的科学发现任务中已展现潜力，但长时序科学分析受限于缺乏基于真实科学数据的过程监督环境——即无法在代理与执行环境交互的过程中逐步验证其分析进度，导致训练信号稀疏，难以引导代理做出可验证的中间分析步骤。

Method: SciDisco由三个核心组件构成：(1) SciThèque——将假设、数据集、隐藏证据图和验证器编译成任务环境，使分析进度可在交互中被检查；(2) DAG-grounded轨迹合成——利用上述环境生成经验证器过滤的多轮演示数据；(3) DiscoPO——将环境作为训练信号来源，对产生可验证分析证据的动作进行逐轮（turn-level）信用分配，实现过程级优化。

Result: SciDisco-14B在假设驱动的科学数据分析基准上达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过构建可过程验证的科学分析环境并提供过程级训练信号，SciDisco有效提升了科学发现代理的长时序推理与假设验证能力，验证了过程监督在真实科学数据分析任务中的可行性。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown promising capabilities in data-driven scientific discovery tasks, where an agent interacts with an execution environment and produces a statistical claim. Long-horizon scientific analysis remains constrained by the lack of process supervised environments over real-world scientific data. This paper introduces SciDisco, a scalable framework for training Scientific Discovery agents in process-verifiable environments. SciThèque compiles hypotheses, datasets, hidden evidence graphs, and verifiers into task environments where analytical progress can be checked during interaction. DAG-grounded trajectory synthesis uses these environments to construct verifier-filtered multi-turn demonstrations. DiscoPO then uses the environment as the source of training signal, assigning turn-level credit to actions that produce verifiable analytical evidence. Experiments show that SciDisco-14B reaches state-of-the-art on hypothesis-driven scientific data analysis benchmarks.

</details>


### [156] [MMShopBench: A Real-Log Benchmark for Multimodal, Multi-Turn Shopping Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29002)
*Zeying Hao,Hao Guo,Mengtao Xu,Yimin Hu,Yuheng Song,Zesheng Zhou,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MMShopBench，这是首个基于真实购物日志构建的多模态、多轮购物智能体基准测试，涵盖图文联合表达的购买意图和强制产品需求标注，并提供离线购物沙盒用于可复现的实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有购物智能体基准大多依赖纯文本或合成请求，未能充分覆盖真实世界中用户通过图像和多轮对话联合表达的复杂购物需求，缺乏能够反映现实场景的多模态多轮购物智能体评估基准。

Method: 从精心清洗和人工标注的购物日志中构建MMShopBench，提供购买意图和强制产品需求的地面真值标注；智能体需从用户图像和多轮对话中联合推断需求，通过图像和文本搜索检索候选产品，并利用产品图像和结构化属性验证候选是否满足所有需求；构建证据驱动的多模态评估协议，配套训练集用于微调开源模型，并搭建离线购物沙盒以确保实验可复现性。

Result: 微调显著缩小了开源模型与领先专有模型之间的性能差距，证明了训练数据的有效性。

Conclusion: MMShopBench填补了多模态多轮购物智能体基准的空白，所构建的训练数据和评估协议能够有效提升开源模型性能，为购物智能体研究提供了可复现的评估平台。

Abstract: Online shoppers increasingly turn to AI shopping assistants, using images and multi-turn dialogue to express and refine product needs that are difficult to articulate in text alone. However, existing benchmarks largely rely on text-only or synthetic requests, underrepresenting complex real-world shopping requirements jointly expressed through images and language. We introduce MMShopBench, the first real-log benchmark for multimodal, multi-turn shopping agents. Built from carefully cleaned and manually annotated shopping logs, MMShopBench provides ground-truth annotations of each request's purchase intent and mandatory product requirements. Agents must infer these requirements jointly from user images and multi-turn dialogue, retrieve candidate products through image and text search, and verify that each candidate satisfies all requirements using its product images and structured attributes. We evaluate representative open-source and proprietary models using an evidence-grounded multimodal protocol and construct a companion training set for fine-tuning an open-source model. To ensure reproducible experimentation, we build an offline shopping sandbox, where fine-tuning substantially narrows the performance gap between our open-source model and leading proprietary models, demonstrating the effectiveness of our training data.

</details>


### [157] [Evidence-Grounded Constraint Checking in Construction Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29058)
*Rashid Mushkani,Hugo Berard,Shin Koseki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于证据的专业文档审阅流水线，用于约束检查问题。该流水线规范化提取的事实、确定性执行四态规则、保留源跨度并升级未解决案例。在160个参考任务的评估中，发现从检索页面概览转向"一页概览+三个重叠瓦片"的证据分配可将项目族标准化决策准确率提高10.6个百分点，但这种区域聚焦优势在更广泛的测试中不持久，且存在分辨率-广度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业文档审阅本质上是一个约束检查问题，决策依赖文本、几何、页面和文档修订之间的关系。现有方法缺乏证据支撑的审阅流程，且关于证据分配的"分辨率-广度"权衡（区域聚焦vs页面广度）尚未得到系统验证。

Method: 提出了一个证据支撑流水线，包括：1) 事实提取与规范化；2) 四态规则的确定性执行；3) 源跨度保留；4) 未解决案例升级。评估采用160个参考任务（来自29个建筑项目），设计了两项测试：重复四系统测试（480张图预算重新分配对比）和不重叠的两系统广度扩展测试。

Result: 重复测试中，将四图像预算从检索页面概览改为"一页概览+三个重叠瓦片"，项目族标准化决策准确率提升10.6个百分点（95% CI: 4.3-18.0，p=0.031）。但该效应在更广泛的块测试中不持久：Region-RAG改变准确率-4.1点（p=0.209），等图像敏感度分析倾向页面广度。精确查找集恢复率低、假通过常见、重复运行一致性校准差。

Conclusion: 证据分配存在分辨率-广度的权衡而非区域聚焦的普适优势。该结果提示需要基于规则的证据路由（rule-aware evidence routing）和专家审查，而非简单偏向区域级证据分配。

Abstract: Professional-document review is a constraint-checking problem in which decisions depend on relations among text, geometry, pages, and document revisions. We present an evidence-grounded pipeline that normalizes extracted facts, executes four-state rules deterministically, retains source spans, and escalates unresolved cases. We evaluate its PDF evidence allocator on 160 reference-based tasks from 29 construction projects using a repeated four-system test and a disjoint two-system breadth extension. In the repeated test, reallocating a four-image budget from retrieved page overviews to one overview and three overlapping tiles improves project-family standardized decision accuracy by 10.6 percentage points (95% project-cluster bootstrap CI: 4.3 to 18.0; exact p = 0.031). This effect does not persist in the broader block: Region-RAG changes accuracy by -4.1 points (95% CI: -10.2 to 1.9; exact p = 0.209), while an equal-image sensitivity favors page breadth. Exact finding-set recovery remains low, false passes remain common, and repeated-run agreement is poorly calibrated. The results identify a resolution-breadth trade-off rather than a universal advantage for region-focused evidence, motivating rule-aware evidence routing and expert review.

</details>


### [158] [On the Generalization of Steering Vectors for Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29062)
*Matthew Nguyen,Kyle Cox,Austin Meek,Iván Arcuschin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了激活引导（activation steering）能否提升思维链（CoT）的忠实性——即让模型在推理中明确承认影响其结论的提示线索。在三个模型（Gemma-3 4B/12B、Qwen-3.5 9B）的提示问答场景中，发现引导仅对最大模型Gemma-3 12B有效；但有效时，效果能跨线索类型和数据集泛化，且效果大小主要由评估设置而非向量训练设置决定；四种向量构建方法效果相似；引导不改变线索使用率，而是减少未被承认的隐藏线索使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着思维链（CoT）被广泛用于提升模型能力，其也成为AI安全监控的重要途径——模型如果能口头化推理过程，就可以被监控。然而模型有时会省略推理中的关键步骤，例如面对暗示错误答案的线索时可能不承认该线索的影响，这种"不忠实的CoT"构成了安全隐患。先前研究表明激活引导可改善CoT忠实性，但对其泛化性、构建方法差异及作用机制尚不清楚，本文旨在系统研究这些问题。

Method: 在提示问答（cued question-answering）设定下，对三个模型（Gemma-3 4B、Qwen-3.5 9B、Gemma-3 12B）进行系统性实验。研究三个维度：(1) 引导效果跨线索类型和数据集（交叉分析）的泛化性；(2)四种不同激活向量构建方法（包括一种优化目标不涉及具体线索的方法）的效果对比；(3) 引导的作用机制——通过比较线索使用率（cue use）与被承认的线索使用来检验引导是促进线索显著性还是直接改变口头化行为。

Result: 激活引导仅在最大的模型（Gemma-3 12B）上稳定提升线索承认率；当引导有效时，其效果跨线索类型和数据集广泛泛化，效果大小主要由评估设置而非训练设置决定；四种向量构建方法效果相近；引导不改变线索使用率，但减少了未被承认的隐藏线索使用，表明引导确实针对口头化行为而非线索显著性。

Conclusion: 激活引导对提升CoT忠实性的有效性在模型规模上存在依赖性（仅对最大模型有效），但一旦有效，其泛化性很强且对构建方法不敏感。引导的作用机制是减少隐藏的线索使用（即减少不承认线索的推理），而非通过提高线索显著性来改变推理本身。这为未来改进CoT忠实性提供了有价值的参考，特别是关于"引导有效时"的泛化特性及其机制理解。

Abstract: Model capabilities have improved in large part due to scaling chain of thought. This has been a promising development for AI safety--where models verbalize their reasoning, it is possible to monitor it. However, in some cases, models do not verbalize important steps in their reasoning process. For example, models prompted with a cue suggesting the incorrect answer may fail to acknowledge that cue, even when it appears instrumental to their conclusion. When chain of thought (CoT) fails to disclose instrumental reasoning steps, we describe it as unfaithful. Prior work has shown that activation steering can be a useful method to improve faithfulness in CoT. We extend this line of work by studying how well steering for faithfulness generalizes across cue types, datasets, and methods of constructing the steering vector for three models (Gemma-3 4B, Qwen-3.5 9B, Gemma-3 12B) in a cued question-answering setting. While steering reliably increases cue acknowledgment for only the largest model (Gemma-3 12B), we find that when steering is effective, its effect generalizes broadly across cue types and datasets--in cross-cue and cross-dataset analyses, effect size is determined primarily by the evaluation setting, rather than the vector's train setting. How the vector is built also matters little--four construction methods, including one whose optimization target mentions no specific cue, yield similar effect sizes. Finally, we consider the possibility that steering promotes the salience of the cue and causes greater cue use, rather than targeting verbalization behaviors. However, we find no evidence for this--steering leaves the rate of cue use roughly unchanged while reducing hidden cue use, i.e., cue use that is not acknowledged.

</details>


### [159] [A Generalized-Bayes Perspective on Counterfactual Explanations: Posterior-Based Decision-Making and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29077)
*Keita Kinjo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明基于距离最小化的反事实解释（CE）在数学上等价于广义贝叶斯框架下使用距离先验的Gibbs后验的最大后验（MAP）估计，并在此基础上提出了DP-GBCE统一框架，引入多种决策规则（贝叶斯决策、CVaR-CE风险规避规则）以及多模型后验混合方法，并定义了评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释通常被表述为距离最小化问题，但该表述的理论基础鲜有研究关注。作者旨在为CE提供严格的贝叶斯理论支撑，并在此基础上拓展出更多决策规则，以应对传统CE方法在理论依据上的不足。

Method: 首先建立距离最小化CE与Gibbs后验MAP估计之间的数学等价性，提出DP-GBCE框架；然后基于后验视角引入两种新决策规则（最小化期望决策损失的贝叶斯决策和风险厌恶的CVaR-CE）；进一步通过贝叶斯模型权重混合多个模型的先验分布以处理模型多重性；最后定义了评估单个CE和后验分布整体的指标。

Result: 在模拟数据和Google Trends数据上进行了实验，量化比较了不同决策规则之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 通过将反事实解释纳入广义贝叶斯框架，本文不仅为距离最小化CE提供了理论基础，还扩展了CE的决策规则和多模型处理能力，使反事实解释在理论上更严谨、在应用上更灵活。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CEs) enhance the interpretability of machine learning models by identifying the smallest change to an input required to obtain a desired output. Although CEs are conventionally formulated as a distance-minimization problem, the theoretical basis of this formulation has received limited attention. We show that a distance-minimization-based CE is mathematically equivalent to the maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimate of a Gibbs posterior within the generalized Bayes framework, specifically when a distance-based prior is used. We call this formulation the Distance-Prior Generalized Bayes CE (DP-GBCE). Building on this posterior perspective, we introduce two decision rules beyond MAP within a unified framework: a Bayes decision that minimizes expected decision loss and CVaR-CE, a risk-averse decision rule. We also propose an extension that uses Bayesian model weights to mix the posterior distributions of multiple models, thereby accounting for model multiplicity, where several models have comparable predictive performance. Finally, we define metrics for evaluating both individual CEs and the posterior distribution as a whole, and use experiments on simulated data and Google Trends data to quantify the trade-offs among the decision rules.

</details>


### [160] [Harnessing the Wisdom of LLM Crowds through Complementarity-Driven Iterative Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29087)
*Yanbin Fang,Xuan Wei,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WILC框架，将多个大语言模型以"接力式互补"方式串联协作，每个后继模型针对前驱输出的特定瓶颈进行诊断与细化，通过双门控机制选择最佳模型过渡，在四个基准上超越单模型自细化、集成和查询路由等方法，并以约1/7的成本达到GPT-5.2的平均基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个LLM存在模型自身的能力边界，而现有方法在部署前固定模型组合方式，忽略了复杂问题求解中模型互补性的动态与状态依赖特征。作者希望从"群体智慧"角度重新构思多LLM协作，使其能够根据当前解题状态动态选择最合适的后续模型。

Method: WILC框架包含两个核心设计原则：(1) 迭代反思-细化（iterative reflection-and-refinement），建立状态保持的工作流程，使模型能诊断并改进先前输出；(2) 互补性驱动的模型选择，通过双门控机制实现——前瞻互补性契合度（PCF）选择最适合当前瓶颈的工人模型，后验互补性增益（PCG）评估所选过渡是否真正改善了演化中的解决方案。

Result: 在四个多样化的基准测试中，WILC优于单模型自细化、集成方法和查询路由方法等现有方案。在标准化定价假设下，WILC以约7倍低的每查询成本达到GPT-5.2的平均基准性能，同时支持自托管部署以促进数据主权。

Conclusion: 本研究将群体智慧理论从静态聚合扩展到序列化AI互补性，为多AI协调提供了可迁移的设计原则，展示了动态状态依赖的模型选择策略在释放集体LLM智能方面的潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in enterprise settings, yet individual models remain bounded by model-specific capability limitations. These heterogeneous boundaries pose a deployment challenge, but also create an opportunity: strategically coordinating multiple LLMs may unlock collective intelligence exceeding any single model. Existing approaches fix how models are combined in advance, overlooking the dynamic, state-dependent role of complementarity in complex problem solving. Drawing on the wisdom-of-crowds paradigm, we reconceptualize collective LLM intelligence as relay-style complementarity: a sequential process in which each successor model is selected to address the specific bottleneck identified in its predecessor's output. To operationalize this, we propose WILC (Wisdom Integration of LLM Crowds), a framework grounded in two design principles. First, iterative reflection-and-refinement establishes a state-preserving workflow through which models diagnose and refine prior outputs. Second, complementarity-driven model selection governs transitions via a dual-gate mechanism: prospective complementarity fit (PCF) identifies the worker most suited to the current bottleneck, while posterior complementarity gain (PCG) evaluates whether the selected transition improves the evolving solution. Experiments across four diverse benchmarks show that WILC outperforms existing approaches, including single-model self-refinement, ensemble methods, and query-routing methods. Under standardized pricing assumptions, WILC matches the average benchmark performance of GPT-5.2 at roughly 7 times lower estimated per-query cost, while facilitating data sovereignty through self-hosted deployment. This study extends wisdom-of-crowds theory from static aggregation to sequential AI complementarity and provides transferable design principles for multi-AI coordination.

</details>


### [161] [CAGE: Certified Authorization under Typed-Return Uncertainty for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29190)
*Blaise Delattre,Cong Wang,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAGE框架，用于认证工具型LLM智能体在绑定扰动（一个离散绑定故障加上有界数值漂移）下动作授权的鲁棒性。CAGE直接认证联合邻域，消除单纯逐通道认证无法覆盖的假允许错误，同时保持高自主决策率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型LLM智能体依赖结构化工具返回值进行授权决策，但现有运行时权限门只对观测到的返回值做点式授权，未保护返回值与其来源绑定过程中可能出现的微小错误（绑定故障及数值漂移）。作者发现分别认证类别通道与数值通道并不能组合成联合安全的保证——各通道单独安全的扰动可能联合导致动作不安全，因此需要直接认证联合邻域的框架。

Method: CAGE框架对联合邻域进行直接认证：对离散分支（绑定故障）进行精确枚举，在每个分支内对连续扰动（数值漂移）进行认证。根据策略是否可执行，提供三种实例化方法：CAGE-Exact直接认证可执行策略本身；CAGE-Lip和CAGE-RS在策略不可执行时，基于显式且有测量的保真度假设认证学习到的门控函数。

Result: 在合成、策略即代码、监管以及真实交易等多个场景中，CAGE消除了精确点式权限门所允许但在预算内的假允许（false allows），同时保留了相当比例的决策可自主执行。实验表明逐通道单独认证的组合确实无法提供联合安全保障，而CAGE能够有效覆盖这一漏洞。

Conclusion: CAGE为工具使用型LLM智能体的运行时授权提供了一种可证明的、针对绑定扰动鲁棒的认证机制。通过直接认证联合邻域而非分别认证各通道，CAGE弥补了点式权限门在授权决策安全上的关键空白，在安全性和自主性之间取得平衡。未来可在更广泛的策略类型和更复杂的扰动模型上进一步扩展。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents act on typed tool returns, records pairing provenance and categorical fields with numerical values. Runtime permission gates generally authorize the observed return and action, leaving the decision unprotected against small errors in how the return was bound to its source. We ask whether a candidate action stays authorized over a declared neighborhood of plausible correctly bound returns: one admissible binding fault plus bounded numerical drift. We prove that certifying the categorical and numerical channels separately does not compose: perturbations that are safe on each channel alone can jointly turn the same action unsafe. CAGE certifies this joint neighborhood directly, enumerating the discrete branches exactly and certifying the continuous perturbation within each branch. Across synthetic, policy-as-code, regulatory, and real-transaction settings, CAGE removes the in-budget false allows that accurate pointwise gates admit, while keeping a useful fraction of decisions autonomous. When the policy is executable, CAGE-Exact certifies the policy itself; otherwise CAGE-Lip and CAGE-RS certify a learned gate under an explicit, measured fidelity assumption.

</details>


### [162] [MirrorCraft: Paired Evaluation under Hidden Rule Changes in Minecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29218)
*Jianxin Gao,Beini Hu,Runze Li,Wanli Peng,Ruohan Lei,Jinyuan Zhang,Linna Deng,Tianyi Yu,Zining Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MirrorCraft，一个用于评估LLM智能体在Minecraft中面对隐藏规则变化时表现的配对基准。每个Mirror世界是其配对Vanilla世界的副本，仅修改选定的服务器端规则。实验表明不同规则套件对智能体表现的影响差异显著，ReAct在无规则描述配置下取得最高Mirror得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Minecraft基准均在固定游戏机制下评估LLM智能体，高表现不能证明智能体在配方、掉落物等熟悉规则发生变化时仍能持续进步。需要一个受控环境来研究智能体面对规则变化时的适应性。

Method: 构建MirrorCraft配对基准：每个Mirror世界复制配对Vanilla世界并仅通过数据包修改选定的服务器端规则，保持地形、生成、资源分布、目标、界面和行动预算不变。包含五个受控生物群系、六套规则集、三个进度目标、两个模型家族和六种智能体配置，统一使用Mineflayer接口。采用确定性进展里程碑和成功率评估任务进度，用规则干预效应（RIE）衡量配对世界间的表现变化。

Result: 隐藏规则变化对不同规则套件的影响差异显著；在无规则描述配置中ReAct取得最高综合Mirror得分；提供精确规则描述仅在平均进展和完成率上带来适度提升（跨三个目标）。

Conclusion: MirrorCraft将Minecraft评估从固定机制扩展到规则变化场景，为研究智能体在规则与熟悉设定不同的世界中如何利用游戏结果进行推理与适应提供了受控实验环境。

Abstract: With the prosperity of the large language models (LLMs), it has become an interesting topic: how do LLM-based agents work in Minecraft? Unfortunately, most existing benchmarks evaluate them under fixed game mechanics. High performance in these settings does not show whether an agent can continue making progress when familiar recipes, drops, and other rules change. In this paper, we introduce MirrorCraft, a paired benchmark for evaluating agents under hidden rule changes in Minecraft. Each Mirror world is a copy of its paired Vanilla world, with selected server-side rules modified by the corresponding datapack. Terrain, spawn, resource placement, objective, interface, and action budget remain matched within every Vanilla-Mirror pair. MirrorCraft includes five controlled biomes, six rule suites, three progression objectives, two model families, and six agent configurations under a shared Mineflayer interface. We evaluate task progress with deterministic advancement milestones and success rate and use the Rule Intervention Effect (RIE) to measure the performance change between matched Vanilla and Mirror worlds. The experiments show that hidden rule changes have strongly different effects across suites. Among the configurations evaluated without rule descriptions, ReAct achieves the highest pooled Mirror score. Providing the exact rules yields modest gains in average progress and completion across all three objectives. MirrorCraft extends Minecraft evaluation beyond fixed mechanics and provides a controlled setting for studying how agents use gameplay outcomes when the rules of the current world differ from familiar ones.

</details>


### [163] [Don't Mix Rewards, Mix Policies: Policy Decomposition and Optimization for Multi-Reward RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29246)
*Ruiming Liang,Yi Zhong,Yizhen Yuan,Yinan Zheng,Tianyi Tan,Tianyue Wang,Haiyun Guo,Jinqiao Wang,Xianyuan Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRISM是一种基于策略空间分解与组合的多奖励强化学习框架，通过优化一组独立的正面策略和一个全局负面策略来缓解多奖励优化中的冲突，同时实现推理时的可控性，在科学推理、工具使用推理和帮助性-安全性对齐任务上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型需要适应不同的人类价值观和使用场景，因此多奖励强化学习变得越来越重要。然而，多奖励优化面临更严重的对齐税问题，不同优化目标之间可能互相权衡甚至冲突，导致后训练不稳定且低效。

Method: PRISM不直接组合不同奖励，而是将策略空间进行分解与组合：优化一组独立的正面策略（每个策略对应一个奖励）和一个全局负面策略。这种方法减轻了多奖励策略优化过程中潜在的冲突，同时在推理阶段通过灵活的策略组合实现可控性。

Result: 在科学推理、工具使用推理和帮助性-安全性对齐三项任务的实验中，PRISM持续优于现有的多奖励强化学习基线，并额外具备推理时的偏好控制能力。

Conclusion: PRISM通过策略空间分解与组合的方式有效解决多奖励RL中的冲突问题，在性能上超越现有基线，并提供了推理时的可控性优势。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) are expected not just to answer correctly, but to adapt their behavior to different human values and use cases. As a result, multi-reward reinforcement learning (RL) has become an increasingly important problem for LLMs, where each reward captures a different aspect of desired behavior. However, optimizing with multiple rewards suffers from a more severe alignment tax issue, where different optimization objectives can trade off or even conflict with each other, leading to unstable and inefficient post-training. In this work, we propose PRISM, a new multi-reward RL framework built upon the idea of policy-space decomposition and composition. Instead of compositing different rewards, PRISM optimizes a set of standalone positive policies and a global negative policy. This alleviates the potential conflict during multi-reward policy optimization, while enabling controllability during inference by flexible policy composition. Experiments on scientific reasoning, tool-use reasoning, and helpfulness-safety alignment show that PRISM consistently outperforms existing multi-reward RL baselines, with extra controllability for inference-time preference control.

</details>


### [164] [Tool Specifications Matter: Uncovering and Mitigating Safety Risks in AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29254)
*Minghui Pan,Jiayuxuan Yang,Yuanyuan Yuan,Yu Jiang,Zhenpeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现schema格式的工具规范是AI智能体安全性能下降的主要来源，并提出SafeKeep——一种推理时安全防护方法，通过将安全判断与工具执行解耦（安全判断使用扁平化文本工具规范，执行保留原始schema格式），在四个LLM上使有害请求的拒绝率从23.8%提升至70.6%，并将提示注入攻击成功率从25.6%降至2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM部署为智能体（AI agent）时安全性显著下降，但此前对其退化根源缺乏深入理解。作者通过白盒表征分析识别出schema格式化的工具规范是导致安全退化的主要原因，这一发现为设计针对性防护措施提供了基础。

Method: 首先进行白盒表征分析，验证schema格式工具规范会削弱模型内部的拒绝信号（refusal signals）。基于此发现，提出SafeKeep推理时防护方法：将安全判断与工具执行解耦——安全评估阶段使用扁平化（flattened）文本格式的工具规范来评估请求，而实际执行阶段仍保留原始的schema格式规范，从而避免schema格式对拒答信号的负面影响。

Result: 在两个代表性基准和四个LLM（涵盖白盒和黑盒模型）上，SafeKeep将有害请求的平均拒绝率从23.8%提升至70.6%，在观察级提示注入下将平均攻击成功率从25.6%降至2.5%，优于现有防护方法且不影响任务处理能力。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了schema格式工具规范是AI智能体安全退化的关键原因，并验证了将安全判断与工具执行解耦的SafeKeep方法能显著提升智能体的安全性，同时保持任务处理能力，为构建更安全的AI智能体系统提供了有效且可落地的方案。

Abstract: AI agents extend large language models (LLMs) with external tools, enabling them to perform complex tasks and translate model outputs into consequential real-world actions. Yet LLMs often become substantially less safe when deployed as agents, and the source of this degradation remains poorly understood. In this paper, we identify schema-formatted tool specifications as a primary source of agent safety degradation and show, through white-box representation analysis, that they weaken the model's internal refusal signals and contribute to unsafe tool execution. Building on this finding, we propose SafeKeep, an inference-time safeguard that decouples safety judgment from tool execution: it assesses requests using flattened textual tool specifications while retaining the original schema-formatted specifications for execution. Across two representative benchmarks and four LLMs, including both white-box and black-box models, SafeKeep increases the average refusal rate for harmful requests from 23.8% to 70.6% and reduces the average attack success rate under observation-level prompt injection from 25.6% to 2.5%. It also outperforms existing safeguards and preserves task-handling capability. We release the code and data at https://github.com/snowcatsmoking/SafeKeep .

</details>


### [165] [MAGA: Multi-Platform Self-Fusion of GUI Agents via Structured Action Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29320)
*Hang Yan,Zhangxuan GU,Beitong Zhou,Jiaxuan Chen,Runze Li,Yusong Hu,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGA框架，通过基于结构化动作正确性重新分配训练信号（抑制无效蒸馏信号、聚焦错误动作），并利用训练时提示优化领域专家教师的监督信号，实现跨移动、网页和桌面环境的统一GUI智能体策略，在8B规模上超越最强基线2.0%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体通常是领域特定的（仅针对移动、网页或桌面单一环境），限制了部署和用户体验。权重合并直接融合领域专家会因专家分歧破坏可执行动作，而在线策略蒸馏（OPD）虽避免教师监督冲突，但在蒸馏过程中对所有响应token一视同仁，忽视了动作token是环境与智能体之间唯一交互接口这一关键特性。

Method: MAGA框架包含两个核心机制：(1) 基于动作结构重分配训练信号——根据生成动作的正确性，抑制不必要或无效的蒸馏信号，将学习重点集中在错误动作上；(2) 训练仅提示（training-only hint）——优化领域特定教师提供的监督信号，同时不改变学生智能体的输入。

Result: 在两种模型规模下，MAGA均取得最高平均成功率。在8B规模下优于最强基线2.0%，且平均性能几乎与教师模型持平。

Conclusion: MAGA通过结构化动作感知的蒸馏信号重分配和训练时提示优化，有效解决了OPD中对所有token一视同仁的问题，实现了跨环境统一GUI智能体策略，显著提升了多环境下的部署性能和用户体验。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents based on large language models are increasingly deployed across mobile, web, and desktop environments. However, existing agents are typically domain-specific, limiting the deployment and user experience. This motivates the consolidation of specialized models into a single cross-environment policy. Weight merging directly merges domain-specific experts but can corrupt executable actions under expert disagreement, while on-policy distillation (OPD) avoids conflicting teacher supervision yet still treats all response tokens equally during distillation, ignoring that action tokens are the only interface between the environment and the agent. To address this, We introduce MAGA that re-allocates training signal according to the structured action. Based on the correctness of the generated action, it suppresses unnecessary or invalid distillation signals and focuses learning on erroneous actions. Besides, a training-only hint optimizes the supervision signal provided by domain-specific teachers without changing the student input. Across two model scales, MAGA achieves the highest mean success rate, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.0% at 8B and achieves almost the same average performance with teachers.

</details>


### [166] [Beyond Component Testing: Validating Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29405)
*Fabio Orazio Mirto,Luca D'Agati,Giuseppe Tricomi,Stefano Silvestri,Francesco Longo,Antonio Puliafito,Giovanni Merlino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综合257篇文献，提出了一个五维分类框架（行为、安全、时间、监管、多智能体）来刻画智能体AI系统的验证问题，并通过三个跨领域案例研究展示了该框架在安全关键场景中的应用，最终提出以生命周期为导向的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统通过多步轨迹（结合规划、工具使用、记忆、交互和适应）运作，其行为依赖于决策在时间上如何展开以及环境条件如何变化，这使传统组件测试和一次性输入-输出评估难以胜任验证需求。现有验证实践未能充分覆盖轨迹级验证的复杂性。

Method: 系统综述257篇论文，覆盖智能体评估、软件保障、信息物理系统、运行时监控和监管指南五个领域。构建五维分类法（行为、安全、时间、监管、多智能体），用于映射现有方法并揭示覆盖缺口；选取医疗、工业运营、智能出行三个安全关键场景进行跨领域案例研究。

Result: 行为评估相对成熟，但时间有效性、运行时证据维护、监管可解释性以及开放式多智能体系统保障仍然发展不足。案例研究证实五个维度在安全关键场景中反复出现，且文献中的失败模式与之相关。

Conclusion: 智能体AI的可信部署取决于在上下文中验证轨迹，而非仅评估孤立组件。研究议程聚焦于：有界自主性规格、对抗性轨迹生成、运行时监控和审计就绪的证据结构四个方向。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems act through multi-step trajectories that combine planning, tool use, memory, interaction, and adaptation. This behavior stretches validation practice beyond component testing and one-shot input--output evaluation, because acceptable system behavior now depends on how decisions unfold over time and under changing environmental conditions. This survey synthesizes 257 papers spanning agent evaluation, software assurance, cyber-physical systems, runtime monitoring, and regulatory guidance in order to characterize the validation problem for agentic systems. The review is organized around a five-dimension taxonomy covering behavioral, safety, temporal, regulatory, and multi-agent concerns, and uses that taxonomy to map current approaches and expose recurrent coverage gaps. The analysis shows that behavioral evaluation is comparatively mature, while temporal validity, runtime evidence maintenance, regulatory legibility, and open-ended multi-agent systems assurance remain under-developed. Three cross-domain case studies (medical care, industrial operations, smart-mobility systems) provide operational illustrations of how the five taxonomy dimensions recur in safety-critical settings, grounded in the failure patterns documented in the reviewed literature. The paper concludes with a lifecycle-oriented research agenda centered on bounded-autonomy specifications, adversarial trajectory generation, runtime monitoring, and audit-ready evidence structures. The central claim is that trustworthy deployment of agentic AI depends on validating trajectories in context rather than assessing isolated components alone.

</details>


### [167] [ModelEquivBench: Certifying Multi-Relational Evaluation of LLM-Generated Optimization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29431)
*Penglin Zhu,Jungang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ModelEquivBench，一个可认证、多关系的评估系统，为LLM生成的优化模型与真实模型的每对比较输出细粒度的语义剖面E0–E6，涵盖模型构建、表示对齐、可行集关系、目标序等价、最优值相等和优化器集合等价，并支持独立可重检的证据（可回放轨迹、精确有理数证书、显式见证）。在三个模型快照上的实验表明其揭示了粗粒度基线无法区分的语义差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成优化模型的评估往往将模型与其真实值简化为单一等价/不等价判定或执行成功率，这些标签既不可独立核查，也无法忠实反映两个公式在多个不同意义上达成一致的现实。

Method: 设计ModelEquivBench：定义E0–E6六级语义关系剖面（模型构建与精确摄入、可验证的表示对齐、同空间与投影可行集关系、目标序等价、最优值相等、优化器集合等价）；为每个判定生成关系适配的可重检证据（可回放轨迹或显式映射用于E0–E1，精确有理数证书用于正向E2–E6，显式见证用于支持的反向结论）；未完成映射搜索、不支持的结构和资源限制输出键入的UNKNOWN或N/A而非猜测，未满足前置条件输出ABSENT。

Result: 在173个基础问题（每模型346个单元）上以无修复协议评估三个模型快照（GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Qwen3.5-397B-A17B）：49、35和25个单元包含可执行候选但在至少一个支持关系上被认证为反例；25、8和18个结构拒绝出现在E2在验证映射下认证映射可行集相等的对上。三个模型在不同剖面阶段失败，无法简化为单一准确率分数。

Conclusion: 粗粒度基线无法表示的模型间差异只能在多关系语义剖面的细粒度下可见；不同模型在剖面不同阶段失败，说明单一准确率指标不足以刻画生成优化模型的质量。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate optimization models from natural language, but existing evaluation often reduces a generated model and its ground truth to a single equivalent/not-equivalent verdict or an execution-success rate--labels that are neither independently checkable nor faithful to the multiple distinct senses in which two formulations can agree. We present ModelEquivBench, a certifying, multi-relational evaluation system that reports a per-pair semantic profile E0--E6: model construction and exact ingestion (E0), verified representation alignment (E1), same-space and projected feasible-set relations (E2, E3), objective-order equivalence (E4), optimal-value equality (E5), and optimizer-set equivalence (E6). Each decided entry carries relation-appropriate, independently re-checkable evidence: replayable traces or explicit maps for E0--E1, exact-rational certificates for positive E2--E6 conclusions, and explicit witnesses for supported negatives.
