Incomplete mapping search, unsupported structure, and resource limits produce typed UNKNOWN or N/A outcomes rather than guesses, while unmet prerequisites are reported as ABSENT. Using ModelEquivBench to evaluate three model snapshots--GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Qwen3.5-397B-A17B--on the same frozen cohort of 173 base problems (346 cells per model) under a no-repair protocol, the resulting profiles expose distinctions that coarse baselines do not represent: 49, 35, and 25 cells contain executable candidates that are nevertheless certified negative on at least one supported relation, and 25, 8, and 18 structural rejections occur on pairs for which E2 certifies mapped feasible-set equality under a verified map. The three model snapshots fail at different stages of the profile and therefore cannot be meaningfully reduced to a single accuracy score.

</details>


### [168] [Beyond Retrieval: Analytic Memory for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29440)
*Zhoujin Tian,Yao Tian,Hao Zhang,Cheng Chen,Yakun Li,Lei Zhang,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaMM框架，将长期多模态记忆从仅支持检索扩展为同时支持检索和分析性记忆（analytic memory）。AdaMM自动从对话、图像和元数据中提取带有溯源链接的属性-值观察数据，发现重复出现的字段结构并进行物化，供过滤、聚合、排序和时间比较等分析操作使用。推理时通过记忆感知规划器将查询分解为检索和分析操作并路由至相应工具。在两个长期多模态记忆基准（MemEye和MemGallery）上分别提升11.3%和7.3%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期多模态记忆系统主要强调"检索记忆"，通过摘要和索引组织交互历史，仅返回与查询相关的信息。然而，长期记忆还需要支持对跨交互累积观察的计算操作（如过滤、聚合、排序、时间比较），这是现有系统缺乏的能力，因此需要一种互补的"分析记忆"抽象。

Method: AdaMM框架包含两个核心机制：（1）离线阶段：不依赖应用定义的schema，自动从对话、图像和上下文元数据中提取带溯源（provenance）链接的属性-值观察，发现重复出现的字段结构并物化，形成可查询的分析记忆；（2）推理阶段：使用记忆感知的规划器（memory-aware planner）将用户查询分解为检索操作和分析操作，并将每个操作路由到对应的工具执行。

Result: 在两个长期多模态记忆基准测试MemEye和MemGallery上的实验表明，AdaMM相较基线分别提升11.3%和7.3%的性能。

Conclusion: AdaMM验证了分析记忆作为检索记忆的补充抽象具有重要价值，通过自动提取结构化属性-值观察并支持查询分解与路由，有效增强了长期多模态记忆系统对累积观察进行计算的能力，而无需依赖预定义的应用schema。

Abstract: Long-term multimodal memory must support not only retrieving relevant information but also computing over observations accumulated across interactions. Existing systems largely emphasize \emph{retrieval memory}, organizing interaction histories through summaries and indexes to return query-relevant information at multiple granularities, from high-level abstractions to underlying records. In this paper, we formulate \emph{analytic memory} as a complementary abstraction that organizes recurring multimodal observations into queryable structures supporting filtering, aggregation, ranking, and temporal comparison. We present AdaMM, a framework that jointly supports retrieval and analytic memory. Rather than relying on application-defined schemas, AdaMM extracts provenance-linked attribute-value observations from dialogue, images, and contextual metadata, discovers recurring field structures, and materializes them for analytical access. At inference time, a memory-aware planner decomposes queries into retrieval and analytic operations and routes each operation to the appropriate tools. Experiments on two long-term multimodal memory benchmarks, MemEye and MemGallery, show that AdaMM improves performance by up to 11.3\% and 7.3\%, respectively.

</details>


### [169] [Self-Play Meets Skill Evolution: Self-Evolving Search Agents that Pose, Solve, and Remember](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29468)
*Zenghuang Fu,Zhaoyang Li,Qiuyuan Ai,Haoyu Wu,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Ante Wang,Guannan He,Changwei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SESA（自进化技能增强智能体），将程序性记忆作为工具增强搜索自博弈的演化状态：挑战者生成问题，求解者检索技能，失败被蒸馏为可复用技能写回记忆，形成"任务生成↔技能记忆"双向协同进化环路。在7个开放域/多跳问答基准上，SESA较SSP平均提升1.2-3.2点，超过SkillRL基线0.9点，且技能库在推理时可选使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自博弈智能体能生成训练问题但缺乏持久状态——失败虽影响梯度却未显式塑造未来练习分布；外部技能记忆虽保留程序性经验但通常从固定任务分布中学习，无法与任务生成形成动态协同。

Method: SESA构建"自我进化"闭环：挑战者提出难题，独立参数化的求解者仅通过技能检索求解；将信息性失败蒸馏为可复用技能写回记忆，更新后的记忆改变求解者行为与成功率，进而反哺挑战者的奖励函数与未来问题分布，形成任务生成与技能记忆的共同演化；检索技能同时塑造在线策略训练轨迹，使技能收益既进入模型参数又可保留于外部记忆中，支持无记忆部署与可选的推理时检索。

Result: 在7个开放域与多跳问答基准上，SESA在多种骨干网络上较SSP平均提升1.2-3.2个准确率点，统一评测协议下超过skill-augmented SkillRL基线0.9点；在Qwen3模型上，SESA-Off较SSP保持1.8-2.2点提升，最终技能库额外贡献0.5-1.0点。

Conclusion: 演化技能记忆不仅是推理时的插件，它改变策略学习过程与未来训练分布，同时保留作为可选外部记忆的价值，验证了程序性记忆与任务生成协同进化的有效性。

Abstract: Self-play agents can generate training problems without questions from target benchmarks, but their curricula lack persistent state: failures affect gradients yet do not explicitly shape future practice. External skill memories preserve procedural experience but are typically learned from fixed task distributions. We introduce \textbf{SESA} (Self-Evolving Skill-Augmented Agent), which makes procedural memory an evolving state of tool-augmented search self-play. A challenger poses problems, while a separately parameterized solver alone retrieves skills. Informative failures are distilled into reusable skills and written back to memory. The updated memory changes solver behavior and success, which changes the challenger's reward and the distribution of future problems; the resulting frontier produces new failures that rewrite memory. This bidirectional loop makes task generation and skill memory co-evolve. Because retrieved skills shape on-policy training trajectories, their benefits can enter the model parameters as well as remain in the external bank, enabling memory-free deployment and optional inference-time retrieval. Across seven open-domain and multi-hop question-answering benchmarks, SESA improves average accuracy over SSP by 1.2--3.2 points across multiple backbones and surpasses the skill-augmented SkillRL baseline by 0.9 points under a unified evaluation protocol. On Qwen3 models, SESA-Off retains 1.8--2.2 points of improvement over SSP, while the final skill bank adds a further 0.5--1.0 points. These results show that evolving skill memory is not merely an inference-time plug-in: it changes policy learning and the future training distribution while retaining value as optional external memory. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zenghuang-Fu/SESA-Self-Evolving-Search-Agents.

</details>


### [170] [AMTFV: Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification for LLM Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29549)
*Rui Zou,Yutao Zhu,Mengqi Wei,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AMTFV（Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification），一种基于数学工具流的智能体验证框架。通过引入"中断-执行-恢复"接口（Mathematical Tool Flow），将验证建模与底层执行解耦，实现精确计算支持，在五个数学推理数据集上超越现有基线方法，最高提升8.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽具备数学问题求解能力，但其候选答案的可靠验证仍具挑战。现有方法存在两大缺陷：基于自然语言反思的方法难以支持精确计算；直接生成验证程序的方法过早地将数学建模与底层实现耦合，限制了验证的灵活性与可靠性。

Method: AMTFV通过引入Mathematical Tool Flow（MTF）作为"中断-执行-恢复"接口，将验证建模与具体执行解耦。验证智能体首先构建验证工作流，将需要可靠执行的数学对象及计算意图编码为MTF请求，发送给数学工具箱智能体；后者解析请求、生成可执行调用并调度至后端进行精确计算。工具输出支持候选答案裁决、答案修订及验证工作流修订。

Result: 在DeepSeek、GPT和Gemini的七种模型配置下，AMTFV在五个数学推理数据集上整体超越代表性基线；在单个模型配置下，相较最强基线的平均准确率最高提升8.3个百分点，且在中等和高验证复杂度样本上获得更大增益。

Conclusion: AMTFV通过工具流解耦验证建模与执行、结合数学工具箱实现精确计算，为LLM数学答案验证提供了一种更可靠且更具适应性的方案，尤其适用于验证复杂度较高的推理场景。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated strong mathematical problem-solving capabilities, yet reliably verifying their candidate answers remains challenging. Existing representative methods mainly revise outputs through natural-language reflection or assist verification by directly generating verification programs; the former may not reliably support exact computation, whereas the latter prematurely couples mathematical modeling with low-level implementation. We propose AMTFV (Agentic Mathematical Tool-Flow Verification). By introducing Mathematical Tool Flow (MTF) as an interrupt--execute--resume interface, AMTFV decouples verification modeling from concrete execution and supports exact computation through a mathematical toolbox. Specifically, the verification agent first constructs a verification workflow, encodes the mathematical objects and computational intent requiring reliable execution in an MTF request, and sends it to the mathematical toolbox agent. The latter parses the request, generates executable calls, and dispatches them to the backend for exact computation. Tool outputs then support candidate-answer adjudication, answer revision, and verification-workflow revision. We evaluate AMTFV on five challenging mathematical reasoning datasets with seven model configurations from DeepSeek, GPT, and Gemini. Experimental results show that AMTFV outperforms the representative baselines evaluated in this study overall; under an individual model configuration, it improves average accuracy over the strongest baseline by up to 8.3 percentage points, with larger gains on samples of medium and high verification complexity.

</details>


### [171] [COntExt: Towards Context-Aware Ontology Extension from Operational Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29553)
*Hussain Hussain,Stefan Schöberl,Angelika Schneider,Verena Geist*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COntExt框架，利用运营指标目录的结构化定义作为上下文来源，自动建议如何将指标中引用的概念和属性集成到现有本体中，将本体扩展问题分解为父类预测、关系类型预测和数据属性分配三个子任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织越来越多地以结构化、机器可读的格式定义运营指标，这些指标定义隐式编码了超出正式本体所捕获范围的领域知识（如概念、属性和关系）。然而，运营指标目录与本体知识之间的连接仍然依赖人工维护，耗时费力且易出错。

Method: 提出COntExt框架，将本体扩展问题形式化为三个子任务：(1)父类预测（parent class prediction）、(2)关系类型预测（relation type prediction）、(3)数据属性分配（data property assignment）。以结构化指标定义作为输入，利用指标上下文信息，在四个网络安全本体上评估多种算法在各子任务上的表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，与仅基于本体上下文的基线方法相比，利用指标派生的上下文在关系类型预测和数据属性分配两个子任务上显著提升了推荐效果。

Conclusion: 运营指标目录是本体扩展中实用但尚未被充分利用的知识来源。该框架能够帮助组织以远低于人工工程的成本维护和扩展本体，实现半自动化的本体演进。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly define operational metrics in structured, machine-readable formats to monitor systems, processes, and compliance. These metric definitions implicitly encode domain knowledge, such as referencing concepts, properties, and relationships, that often extends what is captured in formal ontologies. Yet the connection between operational metric catalogues and ontological knowledge remains manual, ad-hoc, and labor-intensive. We present COntExt, a framework for context-aware ontology extension that takes structured metric definitions as input and suggests how referenced concepts and properties should be integrated into an existing ontology, utilizing the context of these metrics. The framework defines the extension problem as three sub-tasks: parent class prediction, relation type prediction, and data property assignment. Across four cybersecurity ontologies, we evaluate different algorithms for each task. Our results show that metric-derived context improves the suggestions over ontology-context baselines for relation type prediction and data property assignment. Our work demonstrates that operational metric catalogues are a practical and underexploited source for ontology extension. This work enables organizations to maintain their ontologies at a significantly lower cost than manual engineering.

</details>


### [172] [LEMUR: Learning to Align with Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning from Preference Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29559)
*Manith Adikari,Bei Peng,Samuele Vinanzi,Angelo Cangelosi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEMUR是一个将偏好强化学习(PbRL)与多目标强化学习(MORL)相结合的新框架，代理无需预定义奖励函数，即可通过多个人类的偏好反馈同时学习策略和多个目标特定的奖励模型，从而在复杂的多目标决策任务中有效平衡竞争性目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的决策任务通常涉及多个竞争性目标（如性能与效率），但现有MORL方法假设每个目标都有良定义的奖励函数，这在实际中往往难以获得或不可访问；而PbRL虽然能解决无预定义奖励的问题，但大多局限于单目标场景。两者之间存在空白，需要一种能在无预定义奖励函数条件下处理多目标权衡的方法。

Method: 提出LEMUR框架，核心思想是联合学习策略和多个目标特定的奖励模型，从多个人类的偏好反馈中交互式学习。代理通过多个人类提供的偏好信息来推断各目标的奖励函数，并据此学习能有效平衡竞争性目标的多目标策略。

Result: 在多个基准多目标任务上进行了评估，实验结果表明LEMUR在性能上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: LEMUR为解决无预定义奖励函数的多目标决策任务提供了一条有前景的路径，填补了PbRL与MORL之间的研究空白，展示了从人类偏好中学习多目标策略的可行性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) systems are typically trained using a single, well-specified scalar reward function. However, real-world decision-making tasks often involve multiple, competing objectives, such as performance versus efficiency, where ground-truth reward functions are difficult to specify or inaccessible. While Multi-Objective RL (MORL) addresses such trade-offs by modeling rewards as vectors, existing approaches typically assume access to a well-specified reward function for each objective, inheriting the same challenges faced by single-objective RL. Meanwhile, Preference-based RL (PbRL) has shown great potential in solving complex tasks without access to a pre-defined reward function through reward learning from human feedback, yet has largely been studied in single-objective settings. In this work, we bridge this gap with LEMUR: Learning to Align with Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Preference feedback, a novel framework where an agent interactively learns from the preferences of multiple humans to learn optimal multi-objective policies. Our approach jointly learns policies and multiple objective-specific reward models from human feedback, enabling agents to effectively balance competing objectives during learning. We evaluate LEMUR on a variety of benchmark multi-objective tasks, and empirical results demonstrate its superior performance over baseline methods. Our method presents a promising direction for solving multi-objective decision-making tasks without pre-defined reward functions.

</details>


### [173] [DungeonBench: A Benchmark for Rules-Rich Tactical Reasoning in Dungeons & Dragons Combat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29577)
*Ismayil Ismayilov,Atakan Kara,Kaan Oktay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DungeonBench是一个用于《龙与地下城》战斗战术推理的基准测试，覆盖了2014年SRD中大部分可模拟解析的战斗相关内容，提供完整的战术观察、待决决策和可执行选项列表，包含"遭遇战"（单场战斗）和"日常"（多战斗链接）两个赛道，用于评估各种策略在规则丰富环境下的战术推理和资源管理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏与模拟器基准测试在测试规则丰富的战术推理方面存在不足——许多套件无法充分评估"在几何、时机、资源、目标和规则交互同时发挥作用时如何做出好的决策"这一核心能力。现有简化战斗模拟器往往抽象掉关键机制，导致无法全面考验模型的战术推理能力。

Method: DungeonBench基于D&D 5e（2014 SRD）构建：覆盖绝大多数战斗相关的SRD内容，在模拟器中保留被简化模拟器常常省略的复杂机制。每个决策步骤提供完整战术观察、待决决策和可执行选项索引（覆盖移动、攻击、法术、反应、目标和稀缺资源等）。基准测试包含两个赛道：Encounter赛道评估单场战斗的局部战术发挥；Day赛道通过持久生命值、法术位、消耗品、准备和短休息时机将多场战斗链接，迫使策略在即时战术优势与未来生存能力之间权衡。同一引擎生成的决策流支持启发式控制器、语言模型策略、学习型选项排序器和掩码动作强化学习代理。

Result: 对前沿语言模型策略在同一共享决策流上的评估显示：完整的战术观察并未使基准饱和——前沿模型通常在直接遭遇战中获胜，但在链接多场战斗的Day赛道中暴露出资源预算、休息时机和规则感知战术纪律方面的失败。

Conclusion: DungeonBench揭示了现有前沿语言模型在即时战斗指挥方面已有不错表现，但在跨战斗的长期资源管理和规则约束下的策略纪律方面仍有显著不足，说明该基准能有效区分不同层次的战术推理能力，为研究更全面的战术决策模型提供了有价值的评估平台。

Abstract: Games and simulators make valuable benchmarks by turning decisions into measurable outcomes, but many current suites under-test rules-rich tactical reasoning: the ability to choose well when geometry, timing, resources, objectives, and rule interactions all matter at once. We introduce DungeonBench, a benchmark for tactical reasoning in Dungeons & Dragons combat, built to cover the vast majority of combat-relevant 2014 System Reference Document content whose effects can be resolved by the simulator while retaining mechanics that simplified combat simulators often abstract away. At each step, DungeonBench exposes a complete tactical observation, a pending decision, and an indexed list of executable options spanning movement, attacks, spells, reactions, objectives, preparation, and scarce resources. The task is to value legal choices whose consequences depend on action economy, creature traits, battlefield geometry, timing windows, and future encounters. DungeonBench has two tracks: Encounter, which evaluates local tactical play in single fights, and Day, which links encounters through persistent hit points, spell slots, consumables, preparation, and short-rest timing, forcing policies to trade off immediate tactical advantage against future survivability. The same engine-generated decision stream supports heuristic controllers, language-model policies, learned option rankers, and masked-action reinforcement-learning agents. We evaluate frontier language-model policies on this shared decision stream. Results show that full tactical observations do not saturate the benchmark: frontier policies often win direct encounters, but linked encounter days expose failures in resource budgeting, rest timing, and rule-aware tactical discipline.

</details>


### [174] [AgentHPOBench: A Benchmark For Evaluating LLM Agents as Sequential Hyperparameter Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29626)
*Tianyu Huai,Tingshuo Fan,Xinchi Chen,Yining Zheng,Yuxin Wang,Shuang Chen,Jie Zhou,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentHPOBench，一个包含30个可执行机器学习任务的顺序基准，用于评估LLM代理作为自主科学智能体时解释实验证据并引导超参数决策的能力。对12种主流代理和传统HPO基线的统一评估显示，现有代理具备一定实验优化能力，但在持续迭代精炼、复杂日志诊断和向参考性能稳定逼近方面仍存在明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（如静态代码生成、论文复现或最终答案正确性）无法直接评估代理能否解释实验证据并据此做出后续超参数决策。随着LLM从代码补全工具演化为自主科学智能体，需要一种能够顺序评估代理实验优化能力的基准。

Method: 构建AgentHPOBench，包含30个跨7个研究类别的可执行机器学习任务。每个任务以验证过的基线运行开始，代理随后执行多次顺序干预；每一步代理需观察累积的配置、指标和日志，然后提出下一个有效配置。在统一协议下评估12种广泛使用的代理和传统HPO基线。

Result: 当前代理在不同领域表现出可测的实验优化能力，但在持续迭代精炼、复杂日志诊断以及向参考性能持续稳定逼近等方面仍面临明显限制。

Conclusion: AgentHPOBench填补了代理实验能力评估的空白，揭示了现有LLM代理在自主科学实验场景下的能力边界与不足，为未来开发更强大的科学智能体提供了评测基础和改进方向。

Abstract: As LLMs evolve from code completion systems into autonomous scientific agents, evaluating their ability to conduct experiments has become increasingly important. Existing benchmarks typically focus on static code generation, paper replication, or final answer correctness, but do not directly assess whether agents can interpret experimental evidence and use it to guide subsequent hyperparameter decisions. To address this gap, we introduce AgentHPOBench, a sequential benchmark comprising 30 executable machine learning tasks across seven research categories. Each task begins with a validated baseline run, after which an agent performs several sequential interventions. At each step, the agent observes the accumulated configurations, metrics, and logs before proposing the next valid configuration. We evaluate 12 widely used agents and conventional HPO baselines under a unified protocol. The results show that current agents exhibit measurable experimental optimization ability across domains, but still face clear limitations in sustained iterative refinement, complex log diagnosis, and consistent progress toward reported reference performance.

</details>


### [175] [Development of FDD-ON: an Ontology for VAV HVAC System Fault Detection and Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29657)
*Yimin Chen,Brian Fricke,Bo Shen,Jamie Lian,Mingkan Zhang,James Lo,Yun Zhang,Shi Ye,Jiajing Huang,Han Hu,Chujie Lu,Rui Tang,George Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FDD-ON，一个模块化、可扩展的VAV HVAC系统故障检测与诊断（FDD）本体，用于形式化表示系统组件、故障类型、症状状态、故障影响及其属性，并通过明确的因果关系链提供机器可解释的语义框架，旨在解决FDD领域数据可解释性和互操作性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FDD技术对提升HVAC系统可靠性、能效和运维效率至关重要，但实际部署面临数据异构、设备类型多样、诊断输出不一致等挑战。FDD领域缺乏结构化的领域知识来桥接这些差异，导致数据可解释性和互操作性不足，形成信息孤岛，阻碍了FDD实施方案及数字孪生FDD框架、AI驱动维护决策系统等相关应用的落地。

Method: 本文设计了FDD-ON本体，具体方法包括：(1) 构建模块化和可扩展的VAV HVAC系统本体结构；(2) 形式化表示系统组件、故障类型、症状状态、故障影响及关联属性；(3) 集成HVAC系统FDD语义，提供受控词汇表；(4) 建立全面的故障、症状和影响库以覆盖广泛的运行异常及其后果；(5) 通过明确的"致因-故障-症状-影响"关系链提供机器可解释的诊断知识查询基础。

Result: FDD-ON通过公开可用的VAV HVAC系统数据集进行了评估，并在FDD开发应用中进行了演示验证。结果表明FDD-ON为推进跨应用的可扩展、透明、可互操作的FDD解决方案提供了基础性语义框架。

Conclusion: FDD-ON为解决FDD领域的数据互操作性和可解释性问题提供了一个系统性的本体解决方案，能够映射异构FDD输出并支持互操作FDD相关应用的开发，是推动FDD技术在实际建筑中规模化部署的重要基础性工作。

Abstract: Fault detection and diagnosis (FDD) technology is essential for improving HVAC system reliability, energy efficiency, and maintenance effectiveness. However, effective deployment of FDD solutions in buildings requires structured domain knowledge that can bridge heterogeneous data sources, diverse equipment types, and varied diagnostic outputs. Limited data interpretability and interoperability within the FDD domain have led to fragmented information silos, hindering the implementation of FDD and related applications, such as the digital twin-enabled FDD frameworks and artificial intelligence (AI)-driven maintenance decision-making systems. This paper presents an FDD Ontology (FDD-ON), a modular and extensible ontology to formally represent variable air volume (VAV) HVAC system components, fault types, symptom statuses, fault impacts and associated attributes. FDD-ON integrates HVAC system FDD semantics to provide comprehensive representations of fault and symptom attributes, supported by the well-defined controlled vocabulary. Additionally, FDD-ON offers comprehensive fault, symptom, and impact libraries to capture a broad spectrum of operational abnormalities and their consequences in VAV HVAC systems. Through explicit contributing cause-fault-symptom-impact relations, FDD-ON serves as a machine-interpretable basis for querying diagnostic knowledge, mapping heterogeneous FDD outputs, and developing interoperable FDD-related applications. FDD-ON is evaluated using publicly available VAV HVAC system datasets and demonstrated through FDD development applications. Results indicate that FDD-ON provides a foundational semantic framework for advancing scalable, transparent, and interoperable FDD solutions across various applications.

</details>


### [176] [ExtractBench: A Benchmark for Schema-Guided Enterprise Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29677)
*Boyang Zhang,Adrian Lyjak,Eli Stewart,Zhaoqi Li,Simon Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ExtractBench，一个用于企业工作流中schema引导抽取任务的基准测试，首次同时评测值准确率、记录完整性、grounding（来源可追溯性）和成本。该基准包含4,869页、370份企业文档、8个业务领域和67种文档类型，并评估了多种商业VLM和编码代理的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业工作流日益依赖代理进行schema引导抽取（根据用户定义的模式从文档中提取结构化输出并附带来源证据），但现有基准没有同时衡量值准确性、记录完整性、grounding和成本等多个维度，缺乏对实际企业应用场景的全面评估。

Method: 构建包含4,869页、370份企业文档、8个业务领域和67种文档类型的ExtractBench基准。评测体系包括：顺序不敏感的值F1衡量值准确率，词级和页面级F1两条grounding指标衡量来源可追溯性，同时计算衡量成本。标注管道采用三种策略：真实文档用独立系统一致性投票、合成列表用已知值、表单用人工验证。文档带有清晰标签以区分不同挑战场景。

Result: 商业VLM在短文档上表现良好，但在长文档上经常截断记录列表；编码代理准确率更高但成本显著增加。LlamaExtract Agentic Plus在三个指标上均排名第一，其准确率与编码代理相当但成本仅为一小部分。

Conclusion: ExtractBench是首个综合评测schema引导抽取任务多方面指标（值准确率、记录完整性、grounding和成本）的基准，研究揭示了VLM在长文档上的截断问题与编码代理的高成本权衡，并展示了代理型方案在性能与成本之间达到更优平衡的可能性。

Abstract: Enterprise workflows increasingly rely on agents for \emph{schema-guided extraction}: given a document and a user-defined schema, the agent faithfully follows the schema to produce the correct output with source evidence as grounding metadata. We present ExtractBench, a benchmark for schema-guided extraction and, to our knowledge, the first to score value accuracy, record completeness at scale, grounding, and measured cost together. The evaluation system contains 4,869 pages across 370 enterprise documents, 8 business domains, and 67 document types, with clear tags differentiating their challenge scenarios. The scalable schema and ground-truth curation pipeline combines independent-system agreement for real documents, known values for synthetic lists, and human verification for forms. We report order-insensitive value F1 for value accuracy, plus two grounding metrics for source traceability: word- and page-level F1. Commercial VLMs perform well on short documents but often truncate record lists on long ones, while coding agents retain higher accuracy at much higher cost. LlamaExtract Agentic Plus ranks first on all three metrics, with accuracy comparable to coding agents at a fraction of the cost. Dataset and evaluation code are available on \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/llamaindex/ExtractBench}{HuggingFace} and \href{https://github.com/run-llama/ExtractBench}{GitHub}.

</details>


### [177] [Evidence-Ledger Adjudication for Claim-Evidence Traceability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26512)
*Gengyu Chen,Yongjie Yu,Weiling Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究证据账本裁决（evidence-ledger adjudication）方法，即将每项声明与证据数据包配对并分配支持关系，将不支持、矛盾或混合证据的声明退回给作者。在基于AVeriTeC、CLIMATE-FEVER和SciFact构建的2,335行盲测基准上，智能体方法达到0.676的关系准确率和0.601的宏F1，显著优于最佳非智能体基线（0.383准确率、0.303宏F1），并能有效路由矛盾/缺失/混合证据的声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理生成的声明速度远超作者核验引用或检索证据是否支持声明的能力，导致AI辅助写作中证据与声明之间的溯源关系缺乏保障。作者旨在建立一种可审计的追溯层，解决证据与声明之间的断裂问题。

Method: 提出证据账本裁决工作流：将每个声明与证据数据包配对，分配支持关系标签，并将不支持、矛盾或混合证据的声明路由回作者。核心实验基于2,335行的盲测基准，使用独立的Gold标签（来自AVeriTeC、CLIMATE-FEVER、SciFact三个数据集），预测时隐藏金标关系和来源证据标签，仅在评分时联合使用。

Result: 智能体证据账本条件达到0.676关系准确率和0.601宏F1，最佳非智能体基线仅为0.383准确率和0.303宏F1。路由方面，成功路由1270/1435条金标为矛盾、缺证或混合证据的声明，同时路由295/900条受支持的声明（即误路由较受控）。

Conclusion: 证据账本裁决能够将异构证据数据包转化为AI辅助写作的可审计追溯层，显著优于非智能体基线，为AI生成内容的证据可信度提供了系统化的保障机制。

Abstract: AI agents can draft claims faster than authors can check whether the cited or retrieved evidence supports them. We study evidence-ledger adjudication: a claim-evidence traceability workflow that pairs each claim with an evidence packet, assigns a support relation, and routes unsupported, contradicted, or mixed-evidence claims back to the author. The empirical core is a 2,335-row blind benchmark built from independent external labels in AVeriTeC, CLIMATE-FEVER, and SciFact. Gold relations and source evidence labels are hidden during prediction and joined only for scoring. On this benchmark, the agent evidence-ledger condition achieves 0.676 relation accuracy and 0.601 macro-F1, compared with 0.383 accuracy and 0.303 macro-F1 for the best non-agent baseline. It also routes 1270/1435 claims whose gold labels indicate contradiction, missing evidence, or mixed evidence, while routing 295/900 supported claims. These results show that evidence-ledger adjudication can turn heterogeneous evidence packets into an auditable traceability layer for AI-assisted writing.

</details>


### [178] [SVR: Self-Verifying Refinement via Joint Verdict-Confidence Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Test-Time Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28457)
*Hongyu Chen,Liang Lin,Guangrun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SVR（Self-Verifying Refinement）框架，利用无外部反馈的多轮强化学习，让模型通过自我验证信号（正确性判定+置信度）自适应控制测试时计算预算，在七项数学推理基准上以不到3轮推理平均次数超越固定十轮预算的强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 均匀的测试时计算预算在简单输入上浪费计算，而现有验证器引导的方法依赖外部反馈信号。论文旨在探索模型能否将自我验证作为一个内部计算控制策略，实现无外部监督的、适应输入难度的自适应推理预算分配。

Method: SVR采用无oracle的多轮强化学习框架。每轮模型同时生成解答、离散正确性判定和置信度分数；仅当判定为Correct且置信度超过阈值时保留当前答案，否则基于自身自我验证继续优化。真实正确性标签仅用于构建训练奖励（包括解答正确性奖励、校准感知的自我验证奖励和停止就绪正确状态奖励），不暴露于策略的细化提示或推理阶段。使用GRPO在固定时域轨迹上训练，自适应停止仅在推理时激活。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-2B模型上，七个数学推理基准的宏平均准确率达0.563，平均仅需2.99轮推理。在完整系统对比中超越标准GRPO、强多轮基线和固定预算的oracle引导评分反馈参考方法，同时推理轮数显著少于固定十轮推理。

Conclusion: 学习到的自我验证可以作为一种有效的内部控制信号，用于答案保留决策和自适应的测试时计算分配，为无需外部验证器的推理缩放提供了一种可行路径。

Abstract: Scaling test-time computation can improve language-model reasoning, but uniform budgets waste computation on easy inputs, while verifier-guided refinement relies on external feedback. We introduce Self-Verifying Refinement (SVR), an oracle-free multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that learns to use self-verification as a compute-control policy. At each turn, the model produces a solution together with a discrete correctness verdict and a confidence score; it retains the current answer only when the verdict is Correct and confidence exceeds a threshold, and otherwise continues refinement using its own self-verification. Ground-truth correctness is used only to construct training rewards and is never exposed to the policy through refinement prompts or required at inference. SVR is trained with GRPO on fixed-horizon trajectories using rewards that promote solution correctness, calibration-aware self-verification, and stop-ready correct states; adaptive stopping is activated only at inference. On seven mathematical reasoning benchmarks with Qwen3.5-2B, SVR achieves a macro-average accuracy of 0.563 with only 2.99 inference turns on average. In the evaluated complete-system comparison, it exceeds standard GRPO, strong multi-turn baselines, and a fixed-budget oracle-guided score-feedback reference while requiring substantially fewer turns than fixed ten-turn inference. These results demonstrate that learned self-verification can serve as an effective internal control signal for answer retention and adaptive test-time compute allocation.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Global and local error bounds: characterizations via directional derivatives and tangent cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29004)
*Yu Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一般（非凸、非光滑）函数，基于方向导数和切锥建立了全局与局部误差界的全新刻画，并给出了精确的最优常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有误差界理论通常依赖凸性、线性结构或光滑性假设，限制了其在实际非凸非光滑问题中的适用性。作者旨在为一般函数建立不依赖这些假设的误差界刻画条件，并阐明误差界与扰动稳定性、子水平集Hausdorff稳定性等概念之间的深层联系。

Method: 作者在切锥上引入最小单位球面方向导数φ(x)这一关键工具，将其与全局斜率建立精确关系。对Lipschitz函数证明sup φ(x)<0是误差界的充分条件；对凸函数证明该条件也为必要。在有限维情形下，利用φ(x̄)>0判定局部误差界成立且最优常数为1/φ(x̄)；分析φ(x̄)=0时误差界失败的条件，并推导方向导数与到切锥距离的一般估计。

Result: 建立了全局误差界的若干等价条件（适用于一般非凸非光滑函数），将误差界与扰动稳定性、子水平集的Hausdorff稳定性及逆子水平集估计联系起来。对Lipschitz函数给出了误差界的充分条件sup φ(x)<0，对凸函数证明其为充要条件。在有限维中获得了尖锐的局部结果：φ(x̄)>0时局部误差界成立且最优常数为1/φ(x̄)；φ(x̄)=0且存在特定方向时误差界失败。此外还导出了方向导数与到切锥距离的一般估计式。

Conclusion: 该研究为一般（无需凸性或线性结构）函数的全局与局部误差界提供了统一且精确的刻画框架，所得最优常数1/φ(x̄)具有明确的几何解释，为后续在非凸优化、变分分析等领域中的应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop new characterizations of both global and local error bounds for general functions, using directional derivatives and tangent cones without imposing convexity or linear structure. We first establish several equivalent conditions for the global error bound of a nonnegative lower semicontinuous function. These equivalences hold for general, possibly nonconvex and nonsmooth functions. We further link the error bound with perturbation stability, Hausdorff stability of sublevel sets, and an inverse-sublevel-set estimate. Turning to directional derivatives, we introduce the minimal unit-sphere directional derivative \(\varphi(x)\) on the tangent cone and clarify its exact relation with the global slope. For Lipschitz continuous functions we prove that \(\sup_{x\notin S_0} \varphi(x) < 0\) is sufficient for an error bound, and for convex functions on convex sets this condition is also necessary, In finite dimensions we obtain sharp local results: if \(\varphi(\bar{x}) > 0\) at a solution \(\bar{x}\), then a local error bound holds and the optimal constant is exactly \(1/\varphi(\bar{x})\); if \(\varphi(\bar{x}) = 0\) and a suitable direction exists outside the tangent cone of the solution set, the local error bound fails. A general estimate relating the directional derivative to the distance from the tangent cone of the solution set is also derived.

</details>


### [180] [A Policy Iteration Scheme for Semilinear Stochastic Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman Equations with Exponential Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29024)
*Hasib Uddin Molla,Jinniao Qiu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究非马尔可夫随机最优控制问题，其中值函数是由随机Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman（SHJB）方程刻画的随机场。针对不可控随机积分系数导致的半线性SHJB方程的计算困难，提出一种基于逐次线性化的策略迭代算法，将非线性SHJB方程化简为一系列线性方程，并证明该近似序列在均方意义下以指数速率单调收敛于值函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非马尔可夫随机最优控制问题中，值函数是随机场，由SHJB方程刻画。由于存在可测随机性，这类问题相比马尔可夫情形面临更大计算挑战。现有方法难以有效处理半线性SHJB方程，因此需要设计高效的数值算法来解决该计算难题。

Method: 提出一种基于逐次线性化的策略迭代算法。该算法的核心思想是将非线性的半线性SHJB方程逐步转化为一系列的线性SHJB方程，通过迭代求解这些线性方程来逼近原问题的解（即值函数）。同时，对算法的收敛性进行了理论分析。

Result: 理论证明了所提出的策略迭代算法产生的近似序列在均方意义下单调收敛于值函数，且收敛速率是指数级别的。

Conclusion: 该策略迭代算法为非马尔可夫随机最优控制问题提供了一种高效且具有理论保证的求解方案，其单调性和指数收敛速率保证了算法的实用性和稳定性。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the non-Markovian stochastic optimal control problems in which the value function is a random field characterized by a stochastic Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (SHJB) equation. When the stochastic integration coefficients are not controlled, the SHJB equation takes a semilinear form, which is subject to computational challenges compared to the Markovian case due to the measurable randomness. We introduce a policy-iteration algorithm based on successive linearization that reduces the nonlinear SHJB equation to a sequence of linear ones. Furthermore, we prove that the resulting approximation sequence converges monotonically to the value function in the mean-square sense with an exponential rate.

</details>


### [181] [Baseball, An Extensive-Form Game-Theoretic Duel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29041)
*Sebastian E. Ferrando,Eli Kohn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将一个棒球打击回合建模为有限二人零和扩展式博弈（含机会移动与不完美信息），采用序列式表示并通过线性规划求解精确的极小极大均衡，基于MLB Statcast数据估计转移概率，并证明了序贯式最优反应对偶变量分解及状态马尔可夫条件下的倒推法均衡计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 棒球投打对决是一个天然的序列博弈：投手与击球员在不同球数（count）下交替决策，且击球员无法观察投手的实际投球选择（不完美信息）。传统的正规式（normal form）表示在处理这种逐球循环的博弈树时规模爆炸，难以计算精确的极小极大均衡。因此需要一种能高效求解该博弈均衡的建模与计算方法。

Method: 将击球回合建模为带机会移动和不完美信息的有限二人零和扩展式博弈；采用序列式（sequence-form）表示，使线性规划的规模随博弈树线性增长；利用run-expectancy作为效用函数（包含当次得分与半局延续价值）；从MLB Statcast数据估计自然（Nature）的转移概率；推导两个动态规划解释——(1) 一般有限二人零和完美回忆博弈中，序列式最优反应的对偶变量可分解为到达权重与条件延续价值；(2) 在状态马尔可夫假设下，可通过12个非终止球数的倒推计算获得完整极小极大均衡。

Result: 实现了远超正规式可行范围的精确极小极大均衡计算，产生了均衡策略、延续价值，以及非最优动作的战略代价的条件与到达加权测度，并通过倒推法在棒球模型假设下验证了等效解。

Conclusion: 本文提供了棒球投打对决的精确博弈论分析框架，证明了序列式线性规划与动态规划倒推两种计算等价途径的理论基础，为棒球进攻/防守策略的量化评估提供了新工具。

Abstract: We formulate a baseball plate appearance as a finite two-person, zero-sum, extensive-form game with chance moves and imperfect information. Successive pitch cycles are connected through the evolving count, while the Batter chooses an action without observing the Pitcher's current pitch selection. Terminal outcomes are valued through a run-expectancy utility that incorporates both runs scored during the plate appearance and the continuation value of the half-inning. We implement the sequence-form representation of the game, whose linear-programming formulation grows linearly with the game tree and permits exact minimax equilibrium computations for trees far beyond the practical range of the normal form. Nature's transition probabilities are estimated from MLB Statcast data. We also establish two complementary dynamic-programming interpretations. For general finite two-person zero-sum games with perfect recall, we show that the dual variables of the sequence-form best-response programs decompose into reach weights and conditional continuation values. Under the additional state-Markov assumptions of the baseball model, we prove that the full minimax equilibrium can be computed by backward induction over the twelve non-terminal counts. The resulting computations produce equilibrium strategies, continuation values, and conditional and reach-weighted measures of the strategic cost of non-optimal actions.

</details>


### [182] [Newton Method for Fixed-Support Doubly Entropic Wasserstein Barycenter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29109)
*Jianting Pan,Sirong Dai,Lei Yang,Yaomin Wang,Ji'an Li,Ming Yan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究固定支撑的双正则化Wasserstein重心问题，通过熵最优传输的半对偶形式将其转化为光滑无约束凸优化问题，并提出了精确Newton方法及稀疏Newton变体以实现高精度重心计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wasserstein重心在诸多领域有广泛应用，但现有方法在计算精度和可扩展性方面存在不足，需要更高效、高精度的求解算法。

Method: 利用熵最优传输的半对偶公式，将双正则化Wasserstein重心问题重构为对偶变量上的光滑无约束凸优化问题；推导梯度与Hessian的显式表达式，开发精确Newton方法；提出稀疏Newton变体，通过稀疏化传输概率矩阵降低Hessian-向量乘积的计算成本。

Result: 建立了Hessian近似界和收敛性等理论结果；在合成与真实数据集上的实验表明，稀疏Newton方法比（精确方法）收敛更快。

Conclusion: 所提出的精确和稀疏Newton方法能够高效、高精度地求解双正则化Wasserstein重心问题，稀疏变体在保持精度的同时显著提升计算效率。

Abstract: We study the fixed-support doubly regularized Wasserstein barycenter problem. Using the semi-dual formulation of entropic optimal transport, we reformulate the problem as a smooth, unconstrained, convex optimization problem in the dual variables. We then derive explicit expressions for the gradient and Hessian and develop an exact Newton method for high-accuracy barycenter computation. To improve scalability, we propose a sparse Newton variant that sparsifies the transport probability matrices, thereby reducing the cost of Hessian-vector products. We establish theoretical results for the proposed methods, including Hessian approximation bounds and convergence results. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets show that the sparse Newton method converges faster than

</details>


### [183] [Anisotropic Higher-Order Semiregularity of Degenerate Generalized Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29114)
*Tomáš Roubal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出各向异性高阶覆盖与逆估计的自包含三角化表述，适用于光滑映射与广义方程。通过固定的索引目标分解、三角因子条件和三角p-核条件，构造三角p-因子算子，其满射性导出分块的固定尺度包含关系及相应指数逆估计。广义方程情形利用闭凸过程内逼近与光滑算子之和的满射性来实现值域转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为光滑映射和广义方程建立统一且自包含的各向异性高阶覆盖与逆估计理论，通过三角化表述处理不同块上不同阶数的覆盖需求，并允许零块和集合值项。

Method: 对C^p映射固定索引目标分解，施加三角因子条件并选择满足三角p-核条件的方向。构造三角p-因子算子，其满射性导出逐块的固定尺度包含关系（每个非零块Y_i以t^i阶被覆盖）。对于广义方程，提出辅助精确模型定理以隔离值域转移机制（带无环修正纤维），主要充分判据利用闭凸过程内逼近与光滑三角p-因子算子之和的满射性。

Result: 建立了分块的固定尺度包含关系，每个非零块Y_i以阶t^i覆盖，从而在该块上得到指数为1/i的逆估计。光滑结果的导数级分级形式直接从C^p导数验证模型并记录集合值分析所需的分块包含关系。

Conclusion: 该三角化框架统一处理各向异性高阶覆盖问题，允许零块，并成功将集合值项纳入框架以补充缺失方向，为广义方程的逆估计提供了充分的判据。

Abstract: We give a self-contained triangular formulation of anisotropic higher-order
  covering and inverse estimates for smooth mappings and generalized equations.
  For a $C^p$ mapping into a finite-dimensional target, an indexed target
  decomposition is fixed, the corresponding triangular factor condition is
  imposed, and a direction satisfying the associated triangular $p$-kernel
  condition is chosen. Surjectivity of the resulting triangular $p$-factor
  operator yields a fixed-scale inclusion in which each nonzero block $Y_i$ is
  covered at order $t^i$, and hence an inverse estimate with exponent $1/i$ on
  that block. Zero blocks are allowed, and $p$ denotes the highest active grade.
  The smooth result is a derivative-level graded formulation: it verifies the
  model directly from the $C^p$ derivatives and records the blockwise
  fixed-scale inclusion needed for the set-valued analysis.
  For generalized equations, an auxiliary exact-model theorem isolates the
  range-transfer mechanism with acyclic correction fibres. The main sufficient
  criterion uses a closed convex-process inner approximation whose sum with
  the smooth triangular factor operator is surjective, allowing the set-valued
  term to supply missing directions.

</details>


### [184] [Robust Airfoil Design Optimization via a Bilevel Model-Based Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29161)
*Alpaslan Kurt,Dilara Buk,Yusuf Yazicioglu,Figen Oztoprak,Onur Son,Gorkem Demir*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出GLORO，一种针对昂贵函数评估的鲁棒优化双层求解方法，结合贝叶斯优化（参数空间）和局部模型（变量空间），并在RAE2822翼型鲁棒气动设计问题上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒优化问题通常涉及昂贵的目标/约束函数评估，计算成本极高。现有方法难以在保证优化质量的同时控制计算开销，因此需要一种计算高效的求解策略。

Method: GLORO采用双层求解结构：下层在参数空间使用高斯过程代理模型结合贝叶斯优化近似计算鲁棒优化的目标/约束；上层在变量空间利用这些近似评估构造局部模型。两者均旨在将昂贵的函数评估引导至优化过程感兴趣的区域。

Result: 在RAE2822翼型鲁棒设计优化问题上进行了测试，该问题在马赫数和攻角的运行不确定性下优化升阻比的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GLORO能够以计算高效的方式获得满意的鲁棒优化结果，其双层代理策略有效降低了昂贵评估带来的计算负担。

Abstract: We propose GLORO, a bilevel solution methodology for robust optimization involving expensive function evaluations. The methodology is carefully designed to achieve satisfactory optimization results in a computationally efficient manner. It is based on Gaussian Process surrogates in the parameter space for approximate computations of the objective / constraints of the robust optimization problem via Bayesian optimization (lower level), and local models constructed in the variable space using these approximate evaluations (upper level). Both the use of Bayesian optimization (in parameter space) and the use of local models (in variable space) are motivated by the idea of guiding the expensive function evaluations to the regions of interest for the optimization process. The methodological work is motivated by and tested on a robust airfoil design optimization problem. This application focuses on the RAE2822 airfoil, optimizing its shape to ensure a robust lift-to-drag ratio under operational uncertainties in Mach number and angle of attack.

</details>


### [185] [Volume of quasi-homogeneous sublevel sets: Two linear algebra deterministic algorithms with convergence rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29269)
*Didier Henrion,Jean B Lasserre*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出两种确定性算法，用于计算正准齐次多项式单位子水平集的Lebesgue体积。通过降维将高维体积问题转化为一维矩问题，两种算法分别基于Chebyshev逼近和广义特征值问题，给出保证的上下界，且均绕过半定规划仅依赖数值线性代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算正准齐次多项式单位子水平集体积是一个高维难题，传统方法依赖半定规划（SDP），其计算复杂度高且在大规模问题中难以实用。作者旨在通过将高维体积问题降维为一维矩问题，绕过SDP，仅用标准数值线性代数实现高效的确定性上下界计算。

Method: （1）将环境包围盒上的Lebesgue测度通过多项式前推，把高维体积简化为一个一维矩问题，环境维数仅出现在预处理阶段（对稀疏或可分离多项式是多项式时间的）。（2）算法1：用Chebyshev多项式逼近分段常数函数，每个松弛化为快速余弦变换，以多项式速率收敛。（3）算法2：从涉及矩矩阵和局部化矩阵的单个广义特征值问题中提取体积界，矩阵大小随松弛阶线性增长，以指数速率收敛，速率比值由多项式在包围盒上的先验上界决定。（4）两种算法生成的单项式多项式对多元moment-SOS体积层级可行。

Result: 两种算法均返回具有保证的上下界，绕过半定优化，仅依赖标准数值线性代数。算法1以多项式速率收敛，算法2以指数速率收敛。所得多项式对多元moment-SOS层级可行，将其代数与几何收敛速率转移到层级本身，改进了已知最优收敛速率。

Conclusion: 本文通过降维策略和两种无SDP的解法，显著降低了计算高维准齐次多项式子水平集体积的复杂度，并在moment-SOS层级上获得了更优的收敛速率保证。

Abstract: We consider the problem of computing the Lebesgue volume of the unit sublevel set of a positive quasi-homogeneous polynomial. Pushing the Lebesgue measure of an ambient bounding box forward through the polynomial reduces this high-dimensional volume to a one-dimensional moment problem. This removes the ambient dimension from the optimization and confines the dimension to a single preprocessing stage, computing the moments of the polynomial over the box, which is polynomial in the ambient dimension for sparse or separable polynomials. We propose two deterministic algorithms for the resulting univariate relaxations, each returning certified upper and lower bounds on the volume. Both bypass semidefinite optimization entirely and rely only on standard numerical linear algebra. The first approximates a piecewise-constant function by a Chebyshev polynomial, so that each relaxation reduces to a fast cosine transform, and converges at a polynomial rate in the relaxation order. The second extracts the volume bounds from a single generalized eigenvalue problem involving moment and localizing matrices whose size grows linearly with the relaxation order, and converges at an exponential rate; the ratio governing this rate is determined by an a priori upper bound on the polynomial over the bounding box. Finally, the univariate polynomials produced by either algorithm are feasible for the multivariate moment-SOS volume hierarchy. The algebraic and geometric rates therefore transfer to the hierarchy itself, improving on its best known convergence rates.

</details>


### [186] [Rational Jacobi Rotations and the Complexity of Approximating Mixed Integer Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29386)
*Alberto Del Pia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种在固定整数变量个数和Hessian负特征值个数前提下，在Turing机上以多项式时间求解MIQP的epsilon-近似算法，并证明除非P=NP否则两个限制均必要，从而完整刻画MIQP近似的复杂度特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数二次规划（MIQP）的近似求解复杂度尚未被完全刻画。此前已知方法要么在连续情形难以在Turing机上高效求解，要么依赖无理数基变换而无法在Turing机上实现。本文旨在通过新的有理化技术，彻底厘清MIQP近似问题在整数变量个数与Hessian惯性（负特征值个数）两个维度下的复杂度边界。

Method: 核心方法是提出一个多项式时间的同步对角化算法：通过有理变换基底，将给定椭球（以分解形式给出）精确映射为球，同时使目标函数在任意小扰动下可分离，并保持Hessian惯性不变。该方法采用有理Jacobi旋转替代经典的精确可分离构造（后者一般需要无理基且无法在Turing机上实现）。

Result: 证明了在整数变量个数与负特征值个数均为常数时，存在多项式时间的epsilon-近似算法；并证明除非P=NP，两个限制同时必要，因此该结果在MIQP近似复杂度刻画中是最优的（完备的）。该结果在纯连续情形下也是新的。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了MIQP近似问题在整数变量个数与Hessian惯性维度下的复杂度刻画，提出的有理Jacobi旋转和同步对角化算法具有独立价值，为后续在Turing机框架下处理二次规划及其变体提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We present an algorithm that finds an epsilon-approximate solution to a mixed integer quadratic programming (MIQP) problem, and that runs on a Turing machine in time polynomial in the size of the instance and in 1/epsilon, provided that the number of integer variables and the number of negative eigenvalues of the Hessian of the objective function are fixed. Unless P=NP, both restrictions are necessary, so this completes the characterization of the complexity of approximating MIQP in terms of the number of integer variables and the inertia of the Hessian; the result is new already in the purely continuous case. The main ingredient is a polynomial-time simultaneous diagonalization algorithm: it computes a rational change of basis that maps a given ellipsoid, presented in factored form, exactly to a ball, while making the objective function separable up to an arbitrarily small perturbation and preserving the inertia of its Hessian. The classical construction, in which the objective function is made exactly separable, requires a change of basis that is in general irrational, and cannot be carried out on a Turing machine; ours rests instead on rational Jacobi rotations, which we believe to be of independent interest.

</details>


### [187] [Extrapolation-based Direct Search for Nonsmooth Stochastic Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29408)
*Anthony Palmieri,Francesco Rinaldi,Sara Shashaani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于无约束零阶优化的随机直接搜索方法，该方法结合随机采样方向与基于p阶充分下降检验的随机外推线搜索，在条件精度假设下证明了几乎必然收敛到Clarke稳定点，并给出了期望迭代和测试点复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接搜索方法在非光滑随机优化中缺乏外推线搜索的理论保证，已有的随机直接搜索方法要么缺乏收敛性分析，要么不具备外推加速机制。作者旨在弥合这一空白，为结合外推策略的随机直接搜索方法提供严格的收敛性和复杂度理论。

Method: 采用随机轮询方向（random polling directions）与随机外推线搜索（stochastic extrapolating line search）相结合的策略，充分下降检验的阶数为p。理论分析基于条件精度假设，利用超鞅停时（supermartingale stopping-time）论证建立期望迭代复杂度，并推导期望测试点复杂度。

Result: 证明了几乎必然收敛到Clarke稳定点；期望迭代复杂度为O(max{r^{-p}, ε^{-p/(p-1)}})次迭代达到(r,ε)-Goldstein稳定点；期望测试点复杂度为O(ε^{1-n}·max{r^{-p}, ε^{-p/(p-1)}})。在DFO基准测试套件上的数值实验表明该方法与既有随机直接搜索方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对非光滑随机环境中基于外推的直接搜索方法的收敛性和期望复杂度分析，为此类方法的理论保证提供了开创性贡献。实验结果验证了方法的实际有效性。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a stochastic direct-search method for unconstrained zeroth-order minimization of locally Lipschitz, possibly nonsmooth, objectives. The method combines random polling directions with a stochastic extrapolating line search based on a sufficient-decrease test of order $p$. Under conditional accuracy assumptions on the stochastic estimates, we prove almost-sure convergence to Clarke stationary points. We further establish an expected iteration complexity bound. Specifically, using a supermartingale stopping-time argument, we prove that $\mathcal O\left(
  \max\left\{
  r^{-p},
  \varepsilon^{-p/(p-1)}
  \right\}
  \right) $ iterations are sufficient in expectation to reach an $(r,\varepsilon)$-Goldstein stationary point. Moreover, we derive a corresponding expected tested-point complexity bound of order $\mathcal O\bigl(\varepsilon^{1-n} \max\{r^{-p},\varepsilon^{-p/(p-1)}\}\bigr)$. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first convergence and expected-complexity analysis for an extrapolation-based direct-search method in a nonsmooth stochastic setting. Numerical experiments on a DFO benchmark suite highlight competitive performance against well-established stochastic direct-search methods.

</details>


### [188] [When does admission control reduce congestion? A stochastic ordering approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29439)
*Royi Jacobovic,Daniel Stiebel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在客户基于系统状态做出决策（即状态依赖行为）的服务系统中，准入控制是否必然降低拥塞。通过随机序方法，作者证明了由于内生反馈效应，准入控制可能反而无法降低拥塞，并构造了显式反例；同时识别出客户类型与服务需求独立这一条件下，准入控制在初始忙期内有效并能恢复随机占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准入控制是随机服务系统中广泛使用的拥塞调节机制，传统观点认为限制到达可以降低工作负载并改善性能。然而在实际场景中，客户会基于观察到的系统状态做决策，形成控制动作与未来到达之间的反馈闭环。这引出一个根本问题：在状态依赖行为下，准入控制是否必然降低拥塞？现有文献尚未系统回答这一问题。

Method: 采用工作负载过程的随机序（stochastic ordering）作为分析工具。通过构造显式反例展示准入控制失效的现象，进而推导出在初始忙期内准入控制有效的一般性条件，关键在于客户类型与服务需求之间的独立性假设。

Result: 发现准入控制可能因内生反馈效应而无法降低拥塞，并给出了具体反例；证明了当客户类型与服务需求相互独立时，可以消除不利的反馈机制，从而恢复工作负载的随机占优关系，确保准入控制在初始忙期内有效。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了准入控制存在根本性局限，强调了在设计随机服务系统时考虑行为响应（behavioral responses）的重要性。这些发现对服务系统的运维策略设计具有重要的理论和实践指导意义。

Abstract: Admission control is a widely used mechanism for regulating congestion in stochastic service systems, where restricting arrivals is expected to reduce workload and improve performance.
  In many settings, however, customers make decisions based on the observed system state, creating a feedback loop between control actions and future arrivals. This raises a fundamental question: does admission control necessarily reduce congestion under state-dependent behavior?
  We address this question using stochastic ordering of workload processes. We show that admission control may fail to reduce congestion due to endogenous feedback effects, and construct explicit counterexamples illustrating this phenomenon.
  We then identify a general condition under which admission control is effective over the initial busy period. In particular, independence between customers' types and service requirements eliminates the adverse feedback mechanism and restores stochastic dominance.
  These results highlight fundamental limitations of admission control and the importance of accounting for behavioral responses in the design of stochastic service systems.

</details>


### [189] [Robust mean field control: an application to optimal execution under composite uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29514)
*Huafu Liao,Shuhui Liu,Chenchen Mou,Defeng Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个稳健平均场控制框架，用于描述在随机过程和确定性模型参数双重不确定性下的多维最优清算问题，并通过HJBI方程建立了验证结果和适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的最优清算问题同时面临随机过程不确定性（如股价波动）和确定性模型参数不确定性（如模型参数估计误差）。现有的平均场控制框架未能同时处理这两种复合不确定性，且相关HJBI方程的适定性研究在非凸非凹Hamiltonian情况下存在空白。

Method: 采用Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs (HJBI)方程框架，其中变量为概率测度，Hamiltonian非线性地涉及位置与动量的联合分布。通过新的先验估计建立具有一般或二次Hamiltonian（在动量上既非位移凸也非凹）的HJBI方程适定性，并将结果推广到最优清算问题，允许Hamiltonian的导数具有线性增长，求解多维带约束的线性二次最优清算问题。

Result: 建立了HJBI方程在非凸非凹Hamiltonian情形下的适定性（well-posedness），并成功将其应用于多维线性二次最优清算问题，在复合不确定性下求解了带约束的最优清算策略。

Conclusion: 该框架为多维最优清算问题在复合不确定性下提供了严格的数学基础和验证工具，扩展了稳健平均场控制理论的适用范围。

Abstract: We provide a framework for robust mean field control problems that describe multi-dimensional optimal liquidation problems under uncertainty from both the underlying stochastic process and the deterministic model parameters. The verification results are established with Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs (HJBI) equations where the variables are probability measures and the Hamiltonian nonlinearly involves the joint distribution of position and momentum. Using novel a priori estimates, we establish the well-posedness of the HJBI equations featuring general or quadratic Hamiltonians that are neither displacement convex nor concave in their momentum. The a priori estimates and well-posedness results are extended during their application to optimal liquidation problems, where we allow the Hamiltonian to have derivatives of linear growth and solve the constrained multi-dimensional linear quadratic optimal liquidation problem under composite uncertainty.

</details>


### [190] [Physics Matters in PnP: Recovery Guarantees with the MMSE and NN Denoisers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29525)
*Tobias Wolf,Jalal Fadili,Jin Guo,Roy Y. He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对线性不适定问题的前向后向分裂PnP方法，其中去噪器为专门针对（可能退化的）高斯噪声（协方差矩阵可以非对角）设计的MMSE估计器。作者将经典迭代中的部分下降步替换为线性算子，并证明了迭代在逐点和Wasserstein距离下的恢复保证，关键发现是去噪器不能与正向模型无关地选择。结果还推广到神经网络参数化的MMSE去噪器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Plug and Play（PnP）方法的文献中，去噪器通常假设为高斯白噪声或对角协方差，且缺乏对噪声特性与正向模型之间耦合关系的深入分析。作者希望填补这一空白，研究在退化高斯噪声（可能非对角协方差）下，MMSE估计器作为去噪器的PnP方法的理论性质。

Method: 采用前向后向分裂（forward-backward splitting）框架实现PnP迭代。与传统方法不同，作者将下降步中的部分内容替换为一个线性算子，该算子将观测噪声与MMSE估计器的噪声联系起来。在温和假设下推导去噪器的性质，并分别从逐点误差和Wasserstein距离两个角度证明恢复保证。进一步将MMSE去噪器推广为神经网络参数化形式并推导相应恢复界。

Result: 在温和假设下证明了PnP迭代的恢复保证（逐点和Wasserstein距离下）。核心结果表明去噪器不能以物理无关（physics-agnostic）的方式选择，即去噪器必须与正向模型耦合。神经网络参数化MMSE去噪器的情形也获得了相应的恢复界。

Conclusion: 去噪器的设计必须考虑正向观测模型，这为PnP方法提供了重要的理论指导，即不能简单地将预训练的通用去噪器直接应用于任意线性逆问题，而应使其与正向模型及其噪声特性相适应。

Abstract: We investigate the forward-backward-splitting version of the Plug and Play (PnP) method for linear ill-posed problems with MMSE estimators as denoisers. In contrast to existing literature, we consider estimators which are specialized for (degenerate) Gaussian noise with possibly non-diagonal covariance matrices. We further deviate from the classical iteration by replacing parts of the descent step with a linear operator that relates the observation noise to that of the MMSE estimator. Under mild assumptions, we derive several properties of the denoiser and prove recovery guarantees of the iteration both pointwise and in the Wasserstein distance of the underlying probability distributions. Crucially, our analysis shows that the denoiser cannot be chosen in a physics-agnostic way, that is, independently of the forward model. We extend our results to the case where the MMSE denoiser is parametrized by a neural network and derive the corresponding recovery bounds.

</details>


### [191] [Tube MPC for Bilinear Koopman Models using Robust Control Contraction Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29538)
*Thomas de Jong,Mircea Lazar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向双线性Koopman模型的鲁棒管式模型预测控制（MPC）框架，通过推导离散时间鲁棒控制收缩度量（RCCM）来显式处理Koopman模型与真实动力学之间的不匹配，并利用神经网络参数化度量与反馈控制器，实现递归可行性、鲁棒约束满足和输入-状态稳定性（ISS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman算子方法为非线性系统的数据驱动建模提供了线性化表示，但Koopman模型与真实系统之间存在模型失配，这严重限制了模型预测控制在复杂非线性系统上的鲁棒性保证。现有方法缺乏对Koopman模型失配的系统性处理，且高维提升状态空间中的收缩度量合成计算代价高昂。

Method: (1) 为双线性Koopman模型推导离散时间鲁棒控制收缩度量（RCCM），显式量化模型与真实动力学的失配；(2) 提出可扩展的神经网络学习方法，在稀疏采样的提升状态子集上训练度量参数化与关联反馈控制器；(3) 基于所得证书构建带有紧约束的鲁棒管式MPC方案；(4) 利用双线性Koopman模型的线性参数变化结构使MPC问题在固定调度序列下凸化，并通过Chebyshev伪谱离散化将测地线计算重构为一系列二次规划问题。

Result: 在具有状态相关输入增益的非线性摆基准上验证了完整框架的有效性，证明了所提出方法能够实现递归可行性、鲁棒约束满足以及相对于Koopman模型失配的输入-状态稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作为基于Koopman模型的数据驱动鲁棒MPC提供了一套完整的理论保证与可计算实现方案，有效弥合了Koopman线性化表示与真实非线性动力学之间的鲁棒性鸿沟。

Abstract: This paper presents a robust tube model predictive control (MPC) framework for nonlinear systems represented by bilinear Koopman models identified from data. We derive discrete-time robust control contraction metrics (RCCMs) for bilinear Koopman models, which certify a contraction property that explicitly accounts for the mismatch between the Koopman model and the true dynamics. To synthesize such certificates for the typically high-dimensional lifted state, we propose a scalable learning approach that trains neural network parameterizations of the metric and of an associated feedback controller over a sparsely sampled subset of the lifted state space. The resulting certificates are used to construct a robust tube MPC scheme with tightened constraints, for which we establish recursive feasibility, robust constraint satisfaction and input-to-state stability (ISS) of the closed-loop system with respect to the mismatch between the Koopman model and the true dynamics. By exploiting the linear parameter-varying structure of bilinear Koopman models, the MPC problem becomes convex for a fixed scheduling sequence, while the geodesic computation underlying the contractive feedback is reformulated, via a Chebyshev pseudospectral discretization, as a sequence of quadratic programs. The complete framework is validated on a nonlinear pendulum benchmark with a state-dependent input gain.

</details>


### [192] [Node-Wise Dynamic Optimal Control for Evolutionary Games on General Multilayer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29563)
*Rio Aurachman,Giuliano Punzo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在多层网络（multilayer networks）结构群体中促进合作行为的最优激励策略。通过求解Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程，作者获得了在博弈层和策略扩散层构成的网络结构下分配激励的解析解，并基于网络化囚徒困境博弈实现了随时间演化的反馈控制环。研究发现最优激励是节点级的、唯一确定的，且同时包含奖励和惩罚两种形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已在单层网络（generic single-layer networks）的结构化群体中确定了成本最小化意义下的最优去中心化激励策略，但多层网络这一更一般且更具现实意义的情形尚未被充分探索。在现实社会系统中，个体通常通过一种网络结构与邻居博弈互动，同时通过另一层网络参与观点扩散动力学，因此需要针对多层网络的激励策略。

Method: 作者将激励分配问题建模为最优控制问题，通过求解Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程获得解析解。具体实现基于网络化囚徒困境博弈（networked Prisoner's Dilemma Game），构建了一个反馈控制环，使得激励分配随时间动态调整。激励策略依赖于当前合作状态、博弈层网络结构、策略扩散层网络结构以及收益矩阵设计。

Result: 研究发现：（1）最优激励是节点级的（node-wise），每个节点的激励具有唯一性，取决于该节点在博弈层和策略扩散层两个邻接矩阵下的相对位置；（2）最优解并非纯粹的奖励或惩罚，同一时刻不同节点可能分别接受奖励或惩罚，取决于其当前合作水平相对于其他节点的状态；（3）提供了解析解和数值验证，并与现有文献结果进行了对比。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了多层网络群体中最优激励策略的研究空白，为理解网络化社会系统中如何通过动态差异化激励（兼含奖励与惩罚）促进合作提供了理论框架和解析工具，对长期社会系统的可持续性具有重要启示。

Abstract: Promoting cooperative behaviour amongst decision makers has key implications for the long term sustainability of social systems. Incentives can promote cooperation in situations where defection is more favourable. Previous research has identified optimal decentralised incentives in structured populations where the social environment is described by a generic single-layer networks. Optimality is here intended in the sense of cost minimisation. Here, we provide and optimal incentive strategy for structured populations in general network, a rather unexplored case. Further, we look at populations where, through the network structure, each player interact with one set of neighbours while contributing to an opinion diffusion dynamics from a second set of neighbours. Hence we cover the case of multilayer networks. To fill this gap, we provide a solution to the optimal control problem by solving the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation and derive an analytic solution for distributing incentives in multilayer networks. By implementing the dynamics of the networked Prisoner's Dilemma Game, we provide a feedback control loop that yields an optimal incentive distribution over time. The dynamic incentive depends on the current state of cooperation, the game-layer network, the strategy-diffusion layer, and the payoff matrix design. We found that the optimal incentive is node-wise, unique to each node on the network, influenced by its relative position according to the adjacency matrices of both layers. We also found that the optimal solution is not exclusively reward or punishment. While one node can receive a reward, the other node may receive punishment at the same time, depending on its current level of cooperation, relative to other nodes. We provide analytic solutions and numerical validations for the cases studied, comparing our results to the existing literature.

</details>


### [193] [The maximum number of Pareto eigenvalues of a real matrix of order four is 23](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29580)
*Samir Adly*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明任意4×4实矩阵至多有23个不同的Pareto特征值。首先对满足特定条件（主子矩阵实特征值简单且互不重合）的4阶矩阵，利用不动点论证证明Pareto特征值个数为奇数；然后通过支持面形态和三阶主子矩阵特征向量恒等式排除25这一情形；最后通过扰动和不动点指标论证将上界推广到所有4阶实矩阵，并用精确符号计算验证一个显式4阶矩阵恰好有23个不同的正则Pareto特征值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定矩阵Pareto特征值的最大可能个数（即Pareto容量），此前已知4阶的Pareto容量介于23和26之间，需要精确确定该上界。

Method: 先处理满足两个条件（主子矩阵实特征值均单重、不同主子矩阵无公共实特征值）的4阶矩阵，利用不动点论证证明Pareto特征值个数为奇数，从而排除24和26，仅剩需排除25；通过三阶支持面形态和涉及主子矩阵特征向量的恒等式排除25；再利用扰动和不动点指标论证将结论推广到一般4阶实矩阵；最后用精确符号计算验证下界可达。

Result: 证明了所有4×4实矩阵至多有23个不同的Pareto特征值，且构造并验证了一个恰好具有23个不同正则Pareto特征值的显式矩阵，因此4阶Pareto容量为23。

Conclusion: 4×4实矩阵的Pareto特征值个数上界为23，且该上界是可达到的，从而完全确定了4阶Pareto容量。

Abstract: For a given real matrix $A\in\R^{n\times n}$, a Pareto eigenvalue of $A$ is a real number $λ\in\R$ for which there exists a nonzero vector $x\in\R^n\setminus\{0\}$ such that $$ 0\leq x\perp Ax-λx\geq0. $$ We prove that every matrix $A\in\R^{4\times4}$ has at most $23$ distinct Pareto eigenvalues. We first prove the result for matrices of order $4$ satisfying two conditions: every real eigenvalue of a principal submatrix is simple, and two different principal submatrices have no real eigenvalue in common. For these matrices, a fixed point argument shows that the number of Pareto eigenvalues is odd. Previous known results show that the Pareto capacity of order $4$ is between $23$ and $26$. Thus only $25$ remains to exclude. We exclude this case by using the support profiles in order $3$ and identities involving eigenvectors of principal submatrices. A perturbation and fixed point index argument then extends the bound to all real matrices of order $4$. An exact symbolic computation certifies that an explicit matrix of order $4$ has exactly $23$ distinct regular Pareto eigenvalues.

</details>


### [194] [Admissible Set for Linear Systems under Linear State Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29664)
*Jean Lévine,Philipp Rumschinski,Franz Rußwurm,Stefan Streif*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算连续时间线性控制系统在仿射状态约束下可容许集合的内部多面体近似方法，基于障碍理论和线性系统显式解，通过结构化采样直接生成可容许集合的边界样本，避免了传统可行性/可达性方法中对大范围状态空间的离散化，并扩展到Hammerstein系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可行性和可达性方法需要对状态空间进行大规模离散化，计算成本高、可扩展性差。作者旨在开发一种无需离散化大范围状态空间即可直接构造可容许集合内部近似的方法。

Method: 基于障碍理论和线性系统显式解，设计结构化采样程序直接生成可容许集合的边界样本；对每个状态约束分别计算可容许集合并通过交集组合得到整体内近似，避免同时活动约束的显式处理；将边界样本转换为半空间表示以重建多面体。

Result: 在三次积分器和高维质量-弹簧-阻尼链系统上进行了演示，验证了几何构造方法的有效性并展示了向高维系统的可扩展性；同时推导了采样和多面体重建算法的运行时复杂度分析。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种高效、可扩展的计算可容许集合内部多面体近似的框架，避免了大范围状态空间离散化的开销，并能推广到Hammerstein非线性系统。

Abstract: This paper presents a method for computing inner polytopic approximations of admissible sets for continuous-time linear control systems subject to affine state constraints. Building upon barrier theory and the explicit solution of linear systems, a structured sampling procedure is developed to generate boundary samples of the admissible set directly, thereby avoiding the discretization of large regions of the state space required by many viability and reachability methods.
  The proposed framework computes admissible sets for the individual state constraints separately and combines the resulting inner approximations by intersection, avoiding the explicit treatment of simultaneous active constraints. The boundary samples are converted into a half-space representation, and runtime complexity analyses are derived for both the sampling and polytope reconstruction algorithms. An extension of the framework to Hammerstein systems is also presented.
  The proposed approach is demonstrated on a triple integrator and a higher-dimensional mass-spring-damper chain, illustrating the geometric construction of the admissible set approximation and demonstrating scalability to higher-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [195] [ArcLP: A Matlab implementation of an $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{n}L)$ arc-search infeasible interior-point algorithm for linear programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29673)
*Yang,Yaguang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种弧搜索不可行内点算法在Matlab中的实现，用于求解线性规划问题，该算法具有最优的多项式复杂度界限O(√nL)，通过Netlib基准测试验证，其计算性能与广泛使用的Mehrotra预测-校正算法相当甚至更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性规划是运筹学中的核心问题，内点算法是求解大规模LP问题的主流方法之一。虽然弧搜索不可行内点算法理论上拥有最优的多项式复杂度界限，但缺乏高质量、易用的软件实现，作者旨在填补这一空白，提供经过充分测试的Matlab实现，并评估其实际计算性能。

Method: 该算法采用弧搜索（arc-search）策略的不可行内点方法，在每次迭代中沿着椭圆弧逼近中心路径，而非传统的直线搜索方向。作者在Matlab中实现了完整的软件架构，包含核心函数模块，并通过简单示例展示其易用性。算法与Mehrotra预测-校正算法进行了数值对比实验，测试环境覆盖PC和Linux平台，测试数据为Netlib基准库中的标准LP问题。

Result: 在Netlib基准测试中，所有测试问题（包括涉及数万约束和数十万变量的规模）均成功找到最优解。数值结果与Mehrotra预测-校正算法对比表明，该实现不仅在理论上拥有最优的多项式复杂度界限O(√nL)，在实际计算性能上也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：弧搜索不可行内点算法在理论上拥有最佳多项式界的同时，实际计算表现也与流行的Mehrotra预测-校正算法相当，表明该算法在理论与实践之间取得了良好的平衡，是一个值得推广的LP求解工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a Matlab implementation of an arc-search infeasible interior point algorithm for linear programming (LP), which has a proven polynomial bound of $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{n}L)$, the best among all interior-point algorithms for LP. Software architecture and major functions are discussed. Its ease of use is described by a simple example. Crucial strategies are summarized. Quality of the software is assured because this software has been extensively tested on both PC and Linux for the widely used Netlib benchmark linear programming problems in standard form. Some benchmark test problems involve tens of thousands of constraints and hundreds of thousands of variables. For all tested problems, the code found the optimal solution. The numerical results have been compared to those obtained by the popular Mehrotra's predictor-corrector algorithm. We conclude that the implemented algorithm not only has the best polynomial bound but also is computationally competitive compared to the popular Mehrotra's predictor-corrector algorithm.

</details>


### [196] [Sign compression for Muon: SignMuon, MuonSign, and the Limits of Error Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29674)
*Maria Smirnova,Alexey Kravatskiy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究SignMuon优化器——将Muon更新压缩为每参数1比特（取符号）的方法。作者发现对梯度取符号（而非对LMO输出取符号）并不能修复其发散问题，构造了符号放在LMO前（MuonUSign）和两侧（MuonSign）都会上升的实例。误差反馈（Error Feedback）应用于Muon输出时无法拯救SignMuon，但应用于梯度时有效（EF21-MuonUSign和EF21-MuonSign达到O(T^{-1/2})收敛率）。然而实验与理论相反：在集中式CIFAR-10、联邦CIFAR-10和nanoGPT speedrun中，最强的压缩方法恰好是理论上被证明发散的sign-after-LMO，而理论上可收敛的变体表现更差。结论：在这些规模下，启发式的LMO后压缩比理论保证更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索最直接的矩阵感知优化器（Muon）在极低通信预算（每参数1比特）下的压缩方案，并系统考察符号压缩的不同位置（LMO前/后/两侧）以及误差反馈能否修复压缩引起的发散问题，揭示理论与实践之间的张力。

Method: 对Muon更新取逐元素符号得到SignMuon；构造显式小实例证明sign-before和sign-on-both-sides均可能上升；从理论和实验两个维度分析误差反馈（施加于Muon输出或梯度）的效果；在集中式CIFAR-10、联邦CIFAR-10和nanoGPT speedrun上进行基准实验比较各压缩变体。

Result: （1）SignMuon在实践上优于SignSGD，但即使在线性函数上也可能上升；（2）sign-before和sign-both-sides同样会上升，符号围绕oracle的任何放置通常在一般情形都不保证下降；（3）对Muon输出施加误差反馈会失败（对所有平滑常数、步长和动量都失败），但对梯度施加误差反馈有效，EF21变体达到O(T^{-1/2})速率；（4）实验中sign-after-LMO（理论上发散）在三个任务上始终强于理论上收敛的变体。

Conclusion: 压缩位置的选择对实际性能影响巨大，理论上可证明收敛的变体在实践上反而不如被证明发散的启发式方法；在深度学习实际规模下，压缩启发式的有效性比理论收敛保证更具决定性。

Abstract: SignMuon compresses the Muon update to one bit per parameter by taking its elementwise sign, providing the most direct way to run a matrix-aware optimizer under an extremely low communication budget. It outperforms SignSGD in practice, yet it can ascend even on a linear function. Signing the gradient before the Linear Minimization Oracle (LMO), rather than after, does not repair this: we construct a small explicit instance on which sign-before (MuonUSign) and sign-on-both-sides (MuonSign) ascend as well, so no placement of the sign around the oracle descends in general. Error feedback, the standard remedy for a biased compressor, does not rescue SignMuon: when applied to Muon's output, error feedback can fail for every smoothness constant, step size, and momentum. Applied to the gradient, error feedback does work, and EF21-MuonUSign and EF21-MuonSign attain the standard $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ rate for the squared gradient norm on smooth nonconvex problems, the latter at one bit in each direction. Experiments then reverse the ordering: across centralized CIFAR-10, federated CIFAR-10, and the nanoGPT speedrun, the strongest compressed method is consistently sign-after-the-LMO, precisely the placement we prove divergent, with the provably convergent variants trailing it. Compressing after the LMO, a heuristic, matters more at these scales than the guarantee does.

</details>
